





|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau

dasakanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. ānisaṃsavaggau

1. kimatthiyasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kimatthiyāni, bhantai, kusalāni sīlāni kimānisaṃsānī’’ti? ‘‘avippaṭisāratthāni khau, ānanda, kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsānī’’ti|

‘‘avippaṭisārau pana, bhantai, kimatthiyau kimānisaṃsau’’ti? ‘‘avippaṭisārau khau, ānanda, pāmauj‍jatthau pāmauj‍jānisaṃsau’’ti pāmuj‍jatthau pāmuj‍jānisaṃsauti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 11.1|

‘‘pāmauj‍jaṃ pana, bhantai, kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsa’’nti? ‘‘pāmauj‍jaṃ khau, ānanda, pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsa’’nti|

‘‘pīti pana, bhantai, kimatthiyā kimānisaṃsā’’ti? ‘‘pīti khau, ānanda, passaddhatthā passaddhānisaṃsā’’ti|

‘‘passaddhi  pana, bhantai, kimatthiyā kimānisaṃsā’’ti? ‘‘passaddhi khau, ānanda, sukhatthā sukhānisaṃsā’’ti|

‘‘sukhaṃ pana, bhantai, kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsa’’nti? ‘‘sukhaṃ khau, ānanda, samādhatthaṃ samādhānisaṃsa’’nti|

‘‘samādhi pana, bhantai, kimatthiyau kimānisaṃsau’’ti? ‘‘samādhi khau, ānanda, yathābhūtañāṇadassanatthau yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃsau’’ti|

‘‘yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ pana, bhantai, kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsa’’nti? ‘‘yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ khau, ānanda, nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsa’’nti|

‘‘nibbidāvirāgau pana, bhantai kimatthiyau kimānisaṃsau’’ti? ‘‘nibbidāvirāgau khau, ānanda, vimuttiñāṇadassanatthau vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃsau … nisaṃsauti (sī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘iti khau, ānanda, kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisāratthāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsāni; avippaṭisārau  pāmauj‍jatthau pāmauj‍jānisaṃsau; pāmauj‍jaṃ pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsaṃ; pīti passaddhatthā passaddhānisaṃsā; passaddhi sukhatthā sukhānisaṃsā; sukhaṃ samādhatthaṃ samādhānisaṃsaṃ; samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanatthau yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃsau; yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsaṃ; nibbidāvirāgau vimuttiñāṇadassanatthau vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃsau| iti khau, ānanda, kusalāni sīlāni anupubbaina aggāya paraintī’’ti arahattāya pūraintīti (syā॰)| paṭhamaṃ|

2. caitanākaraṇīyasuttaṃ

2. a॰ ni॰ 11.2 ‘‘sīlavatau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampan‍nassa na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘avippaṭisārau mai uppaj‍jatū’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa avippaṭisārau uppaj‍jati| avippaṭisārissa, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘pāmauj‍jaṃ mai uppaj‍jatū’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ avippaṭisārissa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya  karaṇīyaṃ – ‘pīti mai uppaj‍jatū’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ pamuditassa pīti  uppaj‍jati| pītimanassa, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘kāyau mai passambhatū’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ pītimanassa  kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyassa, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘sukhaṃ vaidiyāmī’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati| sukhinau, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘cittaṃ mai samādhiyatū’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitassa, bhikkhavai, na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘yathābhūtaṃ jānāmi passāmī’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati| yathābhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, jānatau passatau na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘nibbindāmi viraj‍jāmī’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ nibbindati viraj‍jati| nibbin‍nassa nibbindassa (sī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, virattassa na caitanāya karaṇīyaṃ – ‘vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ sacchikaraumī’ti| dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yaṃ nibbin‍nau nibbindau (sī॰ ka॰) virattau vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ sacchikarauti|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, nibbidāvirāgau vimuttiñāṇadassanatthau vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃsau; yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsaṃ; samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanatthau yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃsau; sukhaṃ samādhatthaṃ samādhānisaṃsaṃ; passaddhi sukhatthā  sukhānisaṃsā; pīti passaddhatthā passaddhānisaṃsā; pāmauj‍jaṃ pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsaṃ; avippaṭisārau pāmauj‍jatthau pāmauj‍jānisaṃsau; kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisāratthāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsāni| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammā dhammai  abhisandainti, dhammā dhammai paripūrainti apārā pāraṃ gamanāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaupanisasuttaṃ

3. a॰ ni॰ 5.24; 11.3 ‘‘dussīlassa, bhikkhavai, sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti pāmauj‍jaṃ; pāmauj‍jai asati pāmauj‍javipan‍nassa hatūpanisā hauti pīti; pītiyā asati pītivipan‍nassa hatūpanisā hauti passaddhi; passaddhiyā asati passaddhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti sukhaṃ; sukhai asati sukhavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti pāmauj‍jaṃ; pāmauj‍jai sati pāmauj‍jasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nā hauti pīti; pītiyā sati pītisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nā hauti passaddhi; passaddhiyā sati passaddhisampan‍nassa  upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sukhaṃ; sukhai sati sukhasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi pāripūriṃ  gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaupanisasuttaṃ

4. a॰ ni॰ 11.4 tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dussīlassa, āvusau, sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, āvusau, sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti…pai॰ … vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. tatiyaupanisasuttaṃ

5. a॰ ni॰ 11.5 tatra  khau āyasmā ānandau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dussīlassa, āvusau, sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti pāmauj‍jaṃ; pāmauj‍jai asati pāmauj‍javipan‍nassa hatūpanisā hauti pīti; pītiyā asati pītivipan‍nassa hatūpanisā hauti passaddhi; passaddhiyā asati passaddhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti sukhaṃ; sukhai asati sukhavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai asati avippaṭisāravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, āvusau, sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti pāmauj‍jaṃ; pāmauj‍jai sati pāmauj‍jasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nā hauti pīti; pītiyā sati pītisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nā hauti passaddhi; passaddhiyā sati passaddhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sukhaṃ; sukhai sati sukhasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai  sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti avippaṭisārau; avippaṭisārai sati avippaṭisārasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samādhisuttaṃ

6. a॰ ni॰ 11.18 atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī paṭhavisañ‍ñī (sī॰), paṭhavīsañ‍ñī (syā॰) assa, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī assa, na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyausañ‍ñī assa, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī assa, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda, bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau  yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī assa, na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyausañ‍ñī assa, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī assa, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, siyā bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī assa na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyausañ‍ñī assa, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyattanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī assa, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti?

‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, siyā bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī assa, na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī assa, na vāyasmiṃ  vāyausañ‍ñī assa, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī assa, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sāriputtasuttaṃ

7. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā  sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘siyā nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī assa, na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyausañ‍ñī assa, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī  assa, na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī assa, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī assa, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti?

‘‘siyā, āvusau ānanda, bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa…pai॰… na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī assa; sañ‍ñī  ca pana assā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, āvusau sāriputta, siyā bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī assa…pai॰… sañ‍ñī ca pana assā’’ti? ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, āvusau ānanda, samayaṃ idhaiva sāvatthiyaṃ viharāmi andhavanasmiṃ| tatthāhaṃ athāhaṃ (ka॰) tathārūpaṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jiṃ paṭilabhāmi (ka॰) yathā naiva pathaviyaṃ pathavisañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na āpasmiṃ āpausañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na taijasmiṃ taijausañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na vāyasmiṃ vāyausañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na idhalaukai idhalaukasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ, na paralaukai paralaukasañ‍ñī ahausiṃ; sañ‍ñī ca pana ahausi’’nti|

‘‘kiṃsañ‍ñī panāyasmā sāriputtau kiṃ sañ‍ñī panāvusau sāriputta (ka॰) tasmiṃ samayai ahausī’’ti? ‘‘bhavaniraudhau nibbānaṃ bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’’nti khau mai, āvusau, añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sakalikaggissa jhāyamānassa añ‍ñāva ac‍ci uppaj‍jati añ‍ñāva ac‍ci nirujjhati; aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbānaṃ bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’nti  añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati  añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’nti nibbānaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) sañ‍ñī ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, tasmiṃ samayai ahausi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. jhānasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘saddhau ca imasmiṃ vākyai ayaṃ ca kārau natthi syāmapautthakai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca nau (syā॰) aivamuparipi| a॰ ni॰ 8.71 sīlavā; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina  aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā cā’ti! yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau  ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca bahussutau…pai॰… bahussutau ca, nau ca dhammakathikau… dhammakathikau ca, nau ca parisāvacarau… parisāvacarau ca, nau ca visāradau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti… visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau ca vinayadharau… vinayadharau ca, nau ca ārañ‍ñikau ārañ‍ñakau (ka॰) pantasaināsanau… ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, nau ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī… catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, nau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, vinayadharau ca, ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ  diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī assaṃ akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, bahussutau  ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, vinayadharau ca, ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī  hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samantapāsādikau ca hauti sabbākāraparipūrau cā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. santavimaukkhasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘saddhau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca sīlavā…pai॰… sīlavā ca, nau ca bahussutau… bahussutau ca, nau ca dhammakathikau… dhammakathikau ca, nau  ca parisāvacarau… parisāvacarau ca, nau ca visāradau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti… visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau ca vinayadharau… vinayadharau ca, nau ca ārañ‍ñikau pantasaināsanau… ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, nau ca yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā viharati… yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā  tai ca kāyaina phusitvā viharati, nau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, vinayadharau ca, ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, yai  tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai ca kāyaina phusitvā viharaiyyaṃ, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, vinayadharau ca, ārañ‍ñikau ca pantasaināsanau, yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai ca kāyaina phusitvā viharati, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samantapāsādikau ca hauti sabbākāraparipūrau cā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vij‍jāsuttaṃ

10. ‘‘saddhau  ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca sīlavā| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā cā’ti| yatau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca bahussutau bahussutau ca, nau ca dhammakathikau dhammakathikau ca, nau ca parisāvacarau parisāvacarau ca, nau ca visāradau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti visāradau  ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau ca vinayadharau vinayadharau ca, nau ca anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti  sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| anaikavihitañ‍ca…pai॰… pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, nau ca dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti dibbaina ca cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, nau ca āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, vinayadharau ca, anaikavihitañ‍ca pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, dibbaina ca cakkhunā visuddhaina  atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyyaṃ, āsavānañ‍ca khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, vinayadharau ca, anaikavihitañ‍ca pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, dibbaina ca cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ  pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samantapāsādikau ca hauti sabbākāraparipūrau cā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ānisaṃsavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kimatthiyaṃ caitanā ca, tayau upanisāpi ca|

samādhi sāriputtau ca, jhānaṃ santaina vij‍jayāti||


2. nāthavaggau

1. saināsanasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ saināsanaṃ saivamānau bhajamānau nacirassaiva āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti; saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… bhagavā’ti; appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau, samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya; asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī, yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu; āraddhavīriyau  viharati, akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya; thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ nātidūraṃ hauti nāc‍cāsan‍naṃ gamanāgamanasampan‍naṃ divā appākiṇṇaṃ rattiṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghausaṃ appaḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaṃ appaḍaṃsa… siriṃsapasamphassaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰); tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai viharantassa appakasiraina uppaj‍janti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā; tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā  vinayadharā mātikādharā; tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ, imassa kau atthau’ti; tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttāniṃ karaunti anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ hauti| pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ saināsanaṃ saivamānau  bhajamānau nacirassaiva  āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pañ‍caṅgasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau imasmiṃ  dhammavinayai ‘kaivalī vusitavā uttamapurisau’ti vuc‍cati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmacchandau pahīnau hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaikhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ‘kaivalī vusitavā uttamapurisau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kāmacchandau ca byāpādau, thinamiddhañ‍ca bhikkhunau|

uddhac‍caṃ vicikicchā ca, sabbasauva na vij‍jati||

‘‘asaikhaina  ca sīlaina, asaikhaina samādhinā|

vimuttiyā ca sampan‍nau, ñāṇaina ca tathāvidhau||

‘‘sa vai pañ‍caṅgasampan‍nau, pañ‍ca aṅgai pañ‍caṅgāni (syā॰) vivaj‍jayaṃ vivaj‍jiya (ka॰)|

imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, kaivalī iti vuc‍catī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanāni| katamāni dasa? pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni  saṃyaujanāni, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni  saṃyaujanāni? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau, kāmacchandau, byāpādau – imāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa saṃyaujanānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. caitaukhilasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘katamassa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya  padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati…pai॰… saṅghai kaṅkhati… sikkhāya kaṅkhati… sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya  anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti| imassa pañ‍ca  caitaukhilā appahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamassa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati… añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya  anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti| imassa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā haunti|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā imai pañ‍ca  caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina hāyati maṇḍalaina hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā imai  pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni|

‘‘katamassa  pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati, adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai na kaṅkhati…pai॰… saṅghai na kaṅkhati… sikkhāya na kaṅkhati … sabrahmacārīsu na kupitau hauti attamanau na āhatacittau na khilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu na kupitau hauti attamanau na āhatacittau na khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti| imassa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamassa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau susamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai vītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai vītarāgau hauti  …pai॰… na yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati, na añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya…pai॰… daivañ‍ñatarau vāti, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau  susamucchin‍nau hauti| imassa pañ‍ca  caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā haunti|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā imai pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā imai pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. appamādasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñināsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā| appamādau taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.139 aggamakkhāyati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ jaṅgamānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.139 pāṇānaṃ padajātāni, sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ mahantattaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā| appamādau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau  sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭanin‍nā kūṭasamausaraṇā, kūṭau tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā| appamādau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici mūlagandhā, kāḷānusāriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici pupphagandhā, vassikaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici khuddarājānau kuḍḍarājānau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), kuṭṭarājānau, kūṭarājānau (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.53, sabbai tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā bhavanti, rājā taisaṃ cak‍kavattī aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kāci tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā, sabbā tā candappabhāya kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ, candappabhā tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ādic‍cau nabhaṃ abbhussak‍kamānau abbhussuk‍kamānau (sī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.146-148 sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā samuddaṅgamā samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā, mahāsamuddau tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā| appamādau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. āhunaiyyasuttaṃ

16. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai dasa? tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, pac‍caikabuddhau, ubhataubhāgavimuttau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau, kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau, saddhānusārī, dhammānusārī, gautrabhū – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamanāthasuttaṃ

17. dī॰ ni॰ 3.345, 360 ‘‘sanāthā, bhikkhavai, viharatha, mā anāthā| dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anāthau viharati| dasayimai, bhikkhavai, nāthakaraṇā dhammā| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ sātthā sabyañ‍janā (sī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā bahū sutā (?) haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau  kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvacau hauti…pai॰… anusāsaniṃ, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau hauti analasau tatrūpāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ…pai॰… alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti  piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti  paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina  satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā, ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘sanāthā, bhikkhavai, viharatha, mā anāthā| dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anāthau viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa nāthakaraṇā dhammā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyanāthasuttaṃ

18. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sanāthā, bhikkhavai, viharatha, mā anāthā| dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anāthau viharati| dasayimai, bhikkhavai, nāthakaraṇā dhammā| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ‘sīlavā vatāyaṃ bhikkhu pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisū’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ  anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| ‘bahussutau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| ‘kalyāṇamittau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| ‘suvacau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsani’nti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ  mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa  navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni  kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrūpāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| ‘yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu analasau, tatrūpāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātu’nti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau| ‘dhammakāmau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu ‘āraddhavīriyau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisū’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| ‘santuṭṭhau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārainā’ti thairāpi  naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| ‘satimā vatāyaṃ bhikkhu paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa majjhimānukampitassa navānukampitassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ‘pañ‍ñavā vatāyaṃ bhikkhu udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā’ti thairāpi naṃ bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, majjhimāpi bhikkhū… navāpi  bhikkhū vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ  mañ‍ñanti| tassa thairānukampitassa…pai॰… nau parihāni| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘sanāthā, bhikkhavai, viharatha, mā anāthā| dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anāthau viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa nāthakaraṇā dhammā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamaariyāvāsasuttaṃ

19. dī॰ ni॰ 3.348, 360 ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, ariyāvāsā, yai ariyā āvasiṃsu vā āvasanti vā āvasissanti vā| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, aikārakkhau, caturāpassainau, paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau panuṇṇapac‍caikasac‍cau (ka॰), samavayasaṭṭhaisanau, anāvilasaṅkappau, passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau, suvimuttacittau, suvimuttapañ‍ñau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa ariyāvāsā, yai ariyā āvasiṃsu vā āvasanti vā āvasissanti vā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyaariyāvāsasuttaṃ

20. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ  nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰…|

‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, ariyāvāsā, yai ariyā āvasiṃsu vā āvasanti vā āvasissanti vā| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, aikārakkhau, caturāpassainau, paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau, samavayasaṭṭhaisanau, anāvilasaṅkappau, passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau, suvimuttacittau, suvimuttapañ‍ñau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmacchandau  pahīnau hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satārakkhaina caitasā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yāni tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ puthupac‍caikasac‍cāni, saiyyathidaṃ – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti  na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, sabbāni tāni nun‍nāni haunti paṇun‍nāni  nuṇṇāni haunti panuṇṇāni (?) cattāni vantāni muttāni pahīnāni paṭinissaṭṭhāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmaisanā pahīnā hauti, bhavaisanā pahīnā hauti, brahmacariyaisanā paṭippassaddhā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, byāpādasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, vihiṃsāsaṅkappau pahīnau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘rāgau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti, dausau mai pahīnau…pai॰… ‘mauhau  mai pahīnau  ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ ariyā ariyāvāsai āvasiṃsu, sabbai tai imaiva dasa ariyāvāsai āvasiṃsu; yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ ariyā ariyāvāsai āvasissanti, sabbai tai imaiva dasa ariyāvāsai āvasissanti; yai hi yaipi (?) kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi ariyā ariyāvāsai āvasanti, sabbai tai imaiva dasa ariyāvāsai āvasanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa ariyāvāsā, yai ariyā āvasiṃsu vā āvasanti vā āvasissanti vā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

nāthavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍caṅgaṃ, saṃyaujanākhilaina ca|

appamādau āhunaiyyau, dvai nāthā dvai ariyāvāsāti||


3. mahāvaggau

1. sīhanādasuttaṃ

21. ‘‘sīhau, bhikkhavai, migarājā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamati| āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhati| vijambhitvā samantā catuddisaṃ catuddisā (syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.64 anuvilaukaiti| samantā catuddisaṃ catuddisā (syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.64 anuvilaukaitvā  tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadati| tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘māhaṃ khuddakai pāṇai visamagatai saṅghātaṃ āpādaisi’nti!

‘‘‘sīhau’ti, khau bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau  parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, idamassa hauti sīhanādasmiṃ|

ma॰ ni॰ 1.148; vibha॰ 760; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.44 ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| katamāni dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sabbatthagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sabbatthagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikadhātuṃ nānādhātuṃ  laukaṃ anaikadhātunānādhātulaukaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikadhātuṃ nānādhātuṃ laukaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ  hauti…pai॰… brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti…pai॰… brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ  indriyaparaupariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti…pai॰… brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi…pai॰… pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti…pai॰… brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau  pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi  saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| yampi  bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina  saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ  upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau  āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaitī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. adhivuttipadasuttaṃ

22. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yai tai, ānanda, dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ adhivuttipadānaṃ adhimuttipadānaṃ (ka॰) abhiñ‍ñā sacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti, visāradau ahaṃ, ānanda, tattha paṭijānāmi| ‘taisaṃ taisaṃ tathā tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ  yathā yathā paṭipan‍nau santaṃ vā atthīti ñassati, asantaṃ vā natthīti ñassati, hīnaṃ vā hīnanti ñassati, paṇītaṃ vā paṇītanti ñassati, sauttaraṃ vā sauttaranti ñassati, anuttaraṃ vā anuttaranti ñassati; yathā yathā vā pana taṃ ñātaiyyaṃ vā daṭṭhaiyyaṃ  vā sacchikaraiyyaṃ vā, tathā tathā ñassati vā dakkhati vā sacchikarissati vā’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| aitadānuttariyaṃ, ānanda, ñāṇānaṃ yadidaṃ tattha tattha yathābhūtañāṇaṃ| aitasmā cāhaṃ, ānanda, ñāṇā añ‍ñaṃ ñāṇaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā natthīti vadāmi|

‘‘dasayimāni, ānanda, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| katamāni dasa? idhānanda, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampānanda, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau sabbatthagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau anaikadhātuṃ nānādhātuṃ laukaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda  …pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| yampānanda…pai॰… idampānanda…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| yampānanda, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampānanda, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘imāni  khau, ānanda, dasa tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaitī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kāyasuttaṃ

23. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina pahātabbā, nau vācāya| atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā vācāya pahātabbā, nau kāyaina| atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā naiva kāyaina pahātabbā nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā disvā disvā (sī॰ syā॰) pahātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina pahātabbā, nau vācāya? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akusalaṃ āpan‍nau hauti kiñ‍ci daisaṃ kañ‍ci daiva daisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) kāyaina| tamainaṃ anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī aivamāhaṃsu – ‘āyasmā khau akusalaṃ āpan‍nau kiñ‍ci daisaṃ kāyaina| sādhu vatāyasmā kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaitū’ti| sau anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñūhi sabrahmacārīhi vuc‍camānau kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina pahātabbā, nau vācāya|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā vācāya pahātabbā, nau kāyaina? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akusalaṃ āpan‍nau hauti kiñ‍ci daisaṃ vācāya| tamainaṃ anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī aivamāhaṃsu – ‘āyasmā khau akusalaṃ āpan‍nau kiñ‍ci daisaṃ vācāya| sādhu vatāyasmā vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaitū’ti| sau anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñūhi sabrahmacārīhi vuc‍camānau vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā vācāya pahātabbā, nau kāyaina|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā naiva kāyaina pahātabbā nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbā? laubhau, bhikkhavai, naiva kāyaina pahātabbau nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbau| dausau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau … macchariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva kāyaina pahātabbaṃ nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbaṃ|

‘‘pāpikā, bhikkhavai, issā naiva kāyaina pahātabbā nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, pāpikā issā? idha, bhikkhavai, ijjhati gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā dhanaina vā dhañ‍ñaina vā rajataina vā jātarūpaina vā| tatrāñ‍ñatarassa dāsassa vā upavāsassa vā aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatimassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā na ijjhaiyya dhanaina vā dhañ‍ñaina vā rajataina vā jātarūpaina vā’ti| samaṇau vā pana brāhmaṇau vā lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| tatrāñ‍ñatarassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vata ayamāyasmā na lābhī assa cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’nti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpikā issā|

‘‘pāpikā, bhikkhavai, icchā naiva kāyaina pahātabbā nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, pāpikā icchā? vibha॰ 851 idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaddhau samānau ‘saddhauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; dussīlau samānau ‘sīlavāti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; appassutau samānau ‘bahussutauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; saṅgaṇikārāmau samānau ‘pavivittauti  maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; kusītau samānau ‘āraddhavīriyauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; muṭṭhassati samānau ‘upaṭṭhitassatīti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; asamāhitau samānau ‘samāhitauti maṃ  jānaiyyu’nti icchati; duppañ‍ñau samānau ‘pañ‍ñavāti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati; akhīṇāsavau samānau ‘khīṇāsavauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti icchati| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpikā icchā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā naiva kāyaina pahātabbā nau vācāya, pañ‍ñāya disvā pahātabbā|

‘‘tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ laubhau abhibhuyya iriyati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya iriyati| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau abhibhuyya iriyati; nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya iriyatī’ti|

‘‘tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya iriyati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya iriyati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya iriyati; tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau … upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya iriyatī’’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. mahācundasuttaṃ

24. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahācundau caitīsu viharati sahajātiyaṃ| tatra khau āyasmā mahācundau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū  āyasmatau mahācundassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahācundau aitadavauca –

‘‘ñāṇavādaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhamma’nti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau  … kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘bhāvanāvādaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau – ‘bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau  na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau  … macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘ñāṇavādañ‍ca, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau bhāvanāvādañ‍ca – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya  tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau daliddauva samānau aḍḍhavādaṃ vadaiyya, adhanauva samānau dhanavāvādaṃ vadaiyya, abhaugauva samānau bhaugavāvādaṃ vadaiyya| sau kismiñ‍cidaiva dhanakaraṇīyai samuppan‍nai na sak‍kuṇaiyya upanīhātuṃ dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā| tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – ‘daliddauva ayamāyasmā samānau aḍḍhavādaṃ vadaiti, adhanauva ayamāyasmā samānau dhanavāvādaṃ vadaiti, abhaugavāva ayamāyasmā samānau bhaugavāvādaṃ vadaiti| taṃ  kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā kismiñ‍cidaiva dhanakaraṇīyai samuppan‍nai na sak‍kauti upanīhātuṃ dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, ñāṇavādañ‍ca bhikkhu vadamānau bhāvanāvādañ‍ca – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| taṃ cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau abhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; nāyamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā  issā  … pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā abhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘ñāṇavādaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhamma’nti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘ayamāyasmā tathā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ  pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘bhāvanāvādaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau – ‘bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘ñāṇavādañ‍ca, āvusau, bhikkhu vadamānau bhāvanāvādañ‍ca – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ  āyasmantaṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau aḍḍhauva samānau aḍḍhavādaṃ  vadaiyya, dhanavāva samānau dhanavāvādaṃ vadaiyya, bhaugavāva samānau bhaugavāvādaṃ vadaiyya| sau kismiñ‍cidaiva dhanakaraṇīyai samuppan‍nai sak‍kuṇaiyya upanīhātuṃ dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā| tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – ‘aḍḍhauva ayamāyasmā samānau aḍḍhavādaṃ vadaiti, dhanavāva ayamāyasmā samānau dhanavāvādaṃ vadaiti, bhaugavāva ayamāyasmā samānau bhaugavāvādaṃ vadaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā kismiñ‍cidaiva dhanakaraṇīyai samuppan‍nai sak‍kauti upanīhātuṃ dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā’ti|

aivamaivaṃ  khau, āvusau, ñāṇavādañ‍ca bhikkhu vadamānau bhāvanāvādañ‍ca – ‘jānāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, passāmimaṃ dhammaṃ, bhāvitakāyaumhi bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau’ti| tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, dausau… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati, sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau laubhau na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ laubhau nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhati; tathā ayamāyasmā pajānāti yathā pajānatau dausau na hauti… mauhau… kaudhau… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… macchariyaṃ… pāpikā issā… pāpikā icchā na hauti, tathāhimaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya tiṭṭhatī’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kasiṇasuttaṃ

25. dī॰ ni॰ 3.346, 360; a॰ ni॰ 10.29 ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, kasiṇāyatanāni| katamāni dasa? pathavīkasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ; āpaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…pai॰… taijaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… vāyaukasiṇamaikau  sañ‍jānāti… nīlakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… pītakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… lauhitakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… audātakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… ākāsakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa kasiṇāyatanānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kāḷīsuttaṃ

26. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati kuraragharai gulagharai (ka॰) kururagharai mahāva॰ 257 pavattai pabbatai| atha khau kāḷī upāsikā kuraragharikā yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kāḷī upāsikā  kuraragharikā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā kumāripañhaisu –

‘atthassa pattiṃ hadayassa santiṃ,

jaitvāna sainaṃ piyasātarūpaṃ|

aikauhaṃ aikāhaṃ (ka॰) jhāyaṃ sukhamanubaudhiṃ,

tasmā janaina na karaumi sakkhiṃ sakhiṃ (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.161 passitabbaṃ|

sakkhī sakhī (ka॰) na sampaj‍jati kainaci mai’ti||

‘‘imassa  khau, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘pathavīkasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘atthau’ti abhinibbattaisuṃ atthābhinibbattaisuṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| yāvatā khau, bhagini, pathavīkasiṇasamāpattiparamatā, tadabhiñ‍ñāsi bhagavā| tadabhiñ‍ñāya bhagavā assādamaddasa ādimaddasa (sī॰ syā॰) ādīnavamaddasa nissaraṇamaddasa maggāmaggañāṇadassanamaddasa| tassa assādadassanahaitu ādīnavadassanahaitu nissaraṇadassanahaitu maggāmaggañāṇadassanahaitu atthassa patti hadayassa santi viditā hauti|

‘‘āpaukasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini…pai॰… taijaukasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… vāyaukasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… nīlakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… pītakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… lauhitakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… audātakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… ākāsakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇasamāpattiparamā khau, bhagini, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘atthau’ti abhinibbattaisuṃ| yāvatā khau, bhagini, viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇasamāpattiparamatā, tadabhiñ‍ñāsi bhagavā| tadabhiñ‍ñāya bhagavā assādamaddasa ādīnavamaddasa nissaraṇamaddasa maggāmaggañāṇadassanamaddasa| tassa assādadassanahaitu ādīnavadassanahaitu nissaraṇadassanahaitu maggāmaggañāṇadassanahaitu atthassa patti hadayassa santi viditā hauti| iti khau, bhagini, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā kumāripañhaisu –

‘atthassa  pattiṃ hadayassa santiṃ,

jaitvāna sainaṃ piyasātarūpaṃ|

aikauhaṃ jhāyaṃ sukhamanubaudhiṃ,

tasmā  janaina na karaumi sakkhiṃ|

sakkhī na sampaj‍jati kainaci mai’ti||

‘‘imassa  khau, bhagini, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamamahāpañhāsuttaṃ

27. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisiṃsu| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viharathā’ti; mayampi khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ  aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viharathā’ti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau kau adhippayāsau kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ dhammadaisanāya  vā dhammadaisanaṃ anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’’nti?

atha khau tai bhikkhū taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃsu nappaṭik‍kausiṃsu| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  pak‍kamiṃsu – ‘‘bhagavatau  santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisimhā| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamimhā; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudimhā| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdimhā| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā amhai aitadavaucuṃ –

‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viharathāti; mayampi khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – aitha tumhai, āvusau, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viharathāti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau kau adhippayāsau kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ dhammadaisanāya vā dhammadaisanaṃ anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’nti?

‘‘atha  khau  mayaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ  naiva abhinandimhā nappaṭik‍kausimhā| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamimhā – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘aikau, āvusau, pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, dvai pañhā dvai uddaisā dvai vaiyyākaraṇāni, tayau pañhā tayau uddaisā tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇāni, cattārau pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇāni, pañ‍ca pañhā pañ‍cuddaisā pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇāni, cha  pañhā cha uddaisā cha vaiyyākaraṇāni, satta pañhā sattuddaisā satta vaiyyākaraṇāni, aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭhuddaisā aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇāni, nava pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇāni, dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā na caiva sampāyissanti, uttari ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmiṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yau imaisaṃ pañhānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā itau vā pana sutvā|

‘‘‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇa’nti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? aikadhammai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā  viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamasmiṃ aikadhammai? ‘sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā’ – imasmiṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, aikadhammai bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇa’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘dvai pañhā dvai uddaisā dvai vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? dvīsu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu dvīsu? nāmai ca rūpai ca – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, dvīsu dhammaisu  bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘dvai pañhā dvai uddaisā dvai vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘tayau pañhā tayau uddaisā tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tīsu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu tīsu? tīsu vaidanāsu  – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, tīsu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘tayau pañhā tayau uddaisā tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cattārau pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? catūsu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu catūsu? catūsu āhāraisu – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, catūsu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘cattārau pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘pañ‍ca pañhā pañ‍cuddaisā pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? pañ‍casu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu pañ‍casu? pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍casu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘pañ‍ca pañhā pañ‍cuddaisā pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cha  pañhā cha uddaisā cha vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? chasu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu chasu? chasu ajjhattikaisu āyatanaisu – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, chasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘cha  pañhā cha uddaisā cha vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘satta  pañhā sattuddaisā satta vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? sattasu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu sattasu? sattasu viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīsu – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, sattasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘satta pañhā sattuddaisā satta vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭhuddaisā aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? aṭṭhasu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu aṭṭhasu? aṭṭhasu laukadhammaisu – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭhuddaisā aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|
