‘‘‘nava pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? navasu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu navasu? navasu sattāvāsaisu – imaisu  khau, bhikkhavai, navasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī  sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘nava pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? dasasu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu dasasu? dasasu akusalaisu kammapathaisu  – imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, dasasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyamahāpañhāsuttaṃ

28. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kajaṅgalāyaṃ viharati vaiḷuvanai| atha khau sambahulā kajaṅgalakā upāsakā yaina kajaṅgalikā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā kajaṅgalikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kajaṅgalakā upāsakā kajaṅgalikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘vuttamidaṃ, ayyai, bhagavatā mahāpañhaisu – ‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, dvai pañhā dvai uddaisā dvai vaiyyākaraṇāni, tayau pañhā tayau uddaisā tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇāni, cattārau  pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇāni, pañ‍ca pañhā pañ‍cuddaisā pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇāni, cha pañhā cha uddaisā cha vaiyyākaraṇāni, satta pañhā sattuddaisā satta vaiyyākaraṇāni, aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭhuddaisā aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇāni, nava pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇāni, dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti| imassa nu khau, ayyai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘na khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ, napi manaubhāvanīyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ; api ca, yathā maittha khāyati taṃ  suṇātha, sādhukaṃ  manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti, khau kajaṅgalakā upāsakā kajaṅgalikāya bhikkhuniyā pac‍cassausuṃ| kajaṅgalikā bhikkhunī aitadavauca –

‘‘‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇa’nti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? aikadhammai, āvusau, bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamasmiṃ aikadhammai? sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā – imasmiṃ khau, āvusau, aikadhammai bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai  dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇanti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘dvai  pañhā dvai uddaisā dvai vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti iti, khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? dvīsu, āvusau, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu dvīsu? nāmai ca rūpai ca…pai॰…  katamaisu tīsu? tīsu vaidanāsu – imaisu khau, āvusau, tīsu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘tayau pañhā tayau uddaisā tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cattārau pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? catūsu, āvusau, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu catūsu? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu – imaisu khau, āvusau, catūsu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘cattārau pañhā cattārau uddaisā cattāri vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘pañ‍ca  pañhā pañ‍cuddaisā pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? pañ‍casu, āvusau, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu pañ‍casu? pañ‍casu indriyaisu…pai॰… katamaisu chasu? chasu  nissaraṇīyāsu dhātūsu…pai॰… katamaisu sattasu? sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu…pai॰… katamaisu aṭṭhasu? aṭṭhasu ariyaaṭṭhaṅgikamaggaisu – imaisu khau, āvusau, aṭṭhasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭhuddaisā aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘nava  pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? navasu, āvusau, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu navasu? navasu sattāvāsaisu – imaisu khau, āvusau, navasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamānau sammā viraj‍jamānau sammā vimuc‍camānau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘nava pañhā navuddaisā nava vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? dasasu, āvusau, dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| katamaisu dasasu? dasasu kusalaisu kammapathaisu – imaisu  khau, āvusau, dasasu dhammaisu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacittau sammā pariyantadassāvī sammadatthaṃ abhisamaic‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti| ‘dasa pañhā dasuddaisā  dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, āvusau, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitāsu mahāpañhāsu – ‘aikau pañhau aikau uddaisau aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ…pai॰… dasa pañhā dasuddaisā dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’ti, imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ  ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai, āvusau, bhagavantañ‍ñaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau yathā khau (ka॰), yathā nau (bahūsu) a॰ ni॰ 10.115, 172 pana pāṭhabhaidau natthi bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau kajaṅgalakā upāsakā kajaṅgalikāya khau bhikkhuniyā bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā kajaṅgalikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kajaṅgalakā upāsakā yāvatakau ahausi kajaṅgalikāya bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, gahapatayau! paṇḍitā, gahapatayau, kajaṅgalikā bhikkhunī| mahāpañ‍ñā, gahapatayau, kajaṅgalikā bhikkhunī| mañ‍caipi tumhai, gahapatayau, upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi caitaṃ aivamaivaṃ  aivamaiva (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.205 passitabbaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ kajaṅgalikāya bhikkhuniyā byākataṃ| aisau caiva tassa aisau caivaitassa (ma॰ ni॰ 1.205) atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamakausalasuttaṃ

29. ‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, kāsikausalā, yāvatā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa vijitaṃ vijitai (sī॰ ka॰), rājā tattha pasainadi kausalau aggamakkhāyati| rañ‍ñaupi  khau, bhikkhavai, pasainadissa kausalassa atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyā pariharanti disā bhanti viraucamānā, tāva sahassadhā laukau| tasmiṃ sahassadhā laukai sahassaṃ candānaṃ sahassaṃ sūriyānaṃ suriyānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sahassaṃ sinairupabbatarājānaṃ sahassaṃ jambudīpānaṃ sahassaṃ aparagauyānānaṃ sahassaṃ uttarakurūnaṃ sahassaṃ pubbavidaihānaṃ cattāri mahāsamuddasahassāni cattāri mahārājasahassāni sahassaṃ cātumahārājikānaṃ sahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahassaṃ yāmānaṃ sahassaṃ tusitānaṃ sahassaṃ nimmānaratīnaṃ sahassaṃ paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ sahassaṃ brahmalaukānaṃ| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sahassī laukadhātu, mahābrahmā tattha  aggamakkhāyati| mahābrahmunaupi khau, bhikkhavai, atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi  vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ ayaṃ laukau saṃvaṭṭati| saṃvaṭṭamānai, bhikkhavai, laukai yaibhuyyaina sattā ābhassarasaṃvattanikā ābhassaravattanikā (sī॰ syā॰) bhavanti| tai tattha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhaicarā subhaṭṭhāyinau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti| saṃvaṭṭamānai, bhikkhavai, laukai ābhassarā  daivā aggamakkhāyanti| ābhassarānampi khau, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

a॰ ni॰ 10.25 ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, kasiṇāyatanāni| katamāni dasa? pathavīkasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ; āpaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…pai॰… taijaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… vāyaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… nīlakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… pītakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… lauhitakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… audātakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… ākāsakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa kasiṇāyatanāni|

‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ kasiṇāyatanānaṃ yadidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇaṃ aikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| aivaṃsañ‍ñinaupi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃsañ‍ñīnampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.338, 358; a॰ ni॰ 8.64 ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, abhibhāyatanāni| katamāni aṭṭha? ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni; ‘tāni  abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma umāpupphaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ; aivamaivaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma kaṇikārapupphaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ  ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ; aivamaivaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ  lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ; aivamaivaṃ ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma ausadhitārakā audātā audātavaṇṇā audātanidassanā audātanibhāsā, saiyyathā  vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ audātaṃ audātavaṇṇaṃ audātanidassanaṃ audātanibhāsaṃ; aivamaivaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni|

‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ yadidaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni; ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| aivaṃsañ‍ñinaupi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃsañ‍ñīnampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ  atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ  passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā|

‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ yadidaṃ sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| aivaṃpaṭipan‍nāpi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃpaṭipan‍nānampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā| katamā catassau? parittamaikau sañ‍jānāti, mahaggatamaikau sañ‍jānāti, appamāṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti, ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanamaikau sañ‍jānāti – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau sañ‍ñā|

‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ yadidaṃ ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanamaikau sañ‍jānāti| aivaṃsañ‍ñinaupi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃsañ‍ñīnampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, bāhirakānaṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ yadidaṃ ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissāmi, na mai bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃdiṭṭhinau, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘yā cāyaṃ bhavai appaṭikulyatā, sā cassa na  bhavissati; yā cāyaṃ bhavaniraudhai pāṭikulyatā, sā cassa na bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃdiṭṭhinaupi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃdiṭṭhīnampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā paramatthavisuddhiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, paramatthavisuddhiṃ pañ‍ñāpaintānaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tai tadabhiñ‍ñāya tassa sacchikiriyāya dhammaṃ daisainti| aivaṃvādinaupi khau, bhikkhavai, santi sattā| aivaṃvādīnampi khau, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ atthaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ atthi vipariṇāmau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau tasmimpi nibbindati| tasmiṃ nibbindantau aggai viraj‍jati, pagaiva hīnasmiṃ|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñāpainti| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñāpaintānaṃ yadidaṃ chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādā vimaukkhau| aivaṃvādiṃ khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, aivamakkhāyiṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti – ‘samaṇau  gautamau na kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, na  rūpānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, na vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitī’ti| kāmānañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi, rūpānañ‍ca pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi, vaidanānañ‍ca pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi, diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītibhūtau anupādā parinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyakausalasuttaṃ

30. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau uyyaudhikā nivattau hauti vijitasaṅgāmau laddhādhippāyau| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina ārāmau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva ārāmaṃ pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū  abbhaukāsai caṅkamanti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai, bhagavā aitarahi viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ, bhantai, taṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘aisau, mahārāja, vihārau saṃvutadvārau| taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau ālindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaihi; vivarissati tai bhagavā dvāra’’nti|

atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina sau vihārau saṃvutadvārau, taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau ālindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaisi| vivari bhagavā dvāraṃ| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau vihāraṃ pavisitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavatau pādāni mukhaina  ca paricumbati pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘‘rājāhaṃ, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau; rājāhaṃ, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau’’ti|

‘‘kaṃ pana tvaṃ, mahārāja, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau imasmiṃ sarīrai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karausi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaisī’’ti? ‘‘katañ‍ñutaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, katavaiditaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘bhagavā  hi, bhantai, bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa ariyai ñāyai patiṭṭhāpitā yadidaṃ kalyāṇadhammatāya kusaladhammatāya| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa ariyai ñāyai patiṭṭhāpitā yadidaṃ kalyāṇadhammatāya kusaladhammatāya, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā sīlavā vuddhasīlau ariyasīlau kusalasīlau kusalasīlaina saman‍nāgatau| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā sīlavā vuddhasīlau ariyasīlau kusalasīlau kusalasīlaina saman‍nāgatau, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau, arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau, arañ‍ñavanapatthāni  pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau  itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi| ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā  pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpāya kathāya nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpāya kathāya nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī  akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ  nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā  vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā  manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā  micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti, iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, idampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau bhagavati aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaumi, maittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaimi|

‘‘handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma| bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassa dāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ  katvā pak‍kāmīti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sīhādhivutti  kāyaina, cundaina kasiṇaina ca|

kāḷī ca dvai mahāpañhā, kausalaihi parai duvaiti||


4. upālivaggau

1. upālisuttaṃ

31. atha  khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, pātimaukkhaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti?

‘‘dasa khau, upāli, atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, pātimaukkhaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ| katamai dasa? saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya – imai khau, upāli, dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, pātimaukkhaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāsuttaṃ

32. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanā’’ti? ‘‘dasa khau, upāli, pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanā| katamai dasa? pārājikau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, pārājikakathā vippakatā hauti, anupasampan‍nau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau  hauti, anupasampan‍nakathā vippakatā hauti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakakathā vippakatā hauti, paṇḍakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, paṇḍakakathā vippakatā hauti, bhikkhunidūsakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, bhikkhunidūsakakathā vippakatā hauti – imai khau, upāli, dasa pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ubbāhikāsuttaṃ

33. cūḷava॰ 231 ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau’’ti? ‘‘dasahi khau, upāli, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi dasahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti; pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ satthā sabyañ‍janā (sī॰) aivamuparipi kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hauti asaṃhīrau; paṭibalau hauti ubhau atthapac‍catthikai sañ‍ñāpaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ nijjhāpaituṃ paikkhaituṃ pasādaituṃ; adhikaraṇasamuppādavūpasamakusalau  hauti – adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti; adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ jānāti; adhikaraṇaniraudhaṃ  jānāti; adhikaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānāti| imaihi khau, upāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. upasampadāsuttaṃ

34. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabba’’nti? ‘‘dasahi khau, upāli, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ| katamaihi dasahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; pātimaukkhaṃ khau panassa vitthāraina svāgataṃ hauti suvibhattaṃ suppavattaṃ suvinicchitaṃ suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti anabhiratiṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā  vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhisīlai samādapaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhicittai samādapaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhipañ‍ñāya samādapaituṃ| imaihi khau, upāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabba’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. nissayasuttaṃ

35. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nissayau  dātabbau’’ti? ‘‘dasahi khau, upāli, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nissayau dātabbau| katamaihi dasahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; pātimaukkhaṃ khau panassa vitthāraina svāgataṃ hauti suvibhattaṃ suppavattaṃ suvinicchitaṃ suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti anabhiratiṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhisīlai…pai॰… adhicittai… adhipañ‍ñāya samādapaituṃ| imaihi khau, upāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nissayau dātabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sāmaṇairasuttaṃ

36. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘dasahi khau, upāli, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi dasahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; pātimaukkhaṃ khau panassa vitthāraina svāgataṃ hauti suvibhattaṃ suppavattaṃ suvinicchitaṃ suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti anabhiratiṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhisīlai samādapaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhicittai  samādapaituṃ; paṭibalau hauti adhipañ‍ñāya samādapaituṃ| imaihi khau, upāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saṅghabhaidasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘‘saṅghabhaidau saṅghabhaidau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ vinayauti  dīpainti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| tai imaihi dasahi vatthūhi avakassanti apakassanti āvaini āvainiṃ (cūḷava॰ 352) āvaiṇi, āvaiṇikaṃ (tatthaiva adhaulipi) kammāni karaunti āvaini pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| aittāvatā khau, upāli, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṅghasāmaggīsuttaṃ

38. cūḷava॰ 353 ‘‘‘saṅghasāmaggī  saṅghasāmaggī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| tai imaihi dasahi vatthūhi na avakassanti na apakassanti na āvaini kammāni karaunti na āvaini pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| aittāvatā khau, upāli, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamaānandasuttaṃ

39. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘saṅghabhaidau saṅghabhaidau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti…pai॰… pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| tai imaihi dasahi vatthūhi avakassanti apakassanti āvaini kammāni karaunti āvaini pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| aittāvatā khau, ānanda, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti|

‘‘samaggaṃ pana, bhantai, saṅghaṃ bhinditvā kiṃ sau pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘kappaṭṭhikaṃ, ānanda, kibbisaṃ pasavatī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, kappaṭṭhikaṃ kibbisa’’nti? ‘‘kappaṃ, ānanda, nirayamhi pac‍catīti –

‘‘āpāyikau  nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau saṅghabhaidakau|

vaggaratau adhammaṭṭhau, yaugakkhaimā padhaṃsati|

saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ bhinditvā bhaitvāna (sī॰ syā॰), bhitvāna (ka॰) cūḷava॰ 354; itivu॰ 18; kathāva॰ 657 kappaṃ nirayamhi pac‍catī’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyaānandasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘‘saṅghasāmaggī  saṅghasāmaggī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| tai imaihi dasahi vatthūhi  na avakassanti na apakassanti na āvaini kammāni karaunti na āvaini pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| aittāvatā khau, ānanda, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti|

‘‘bhin‍naṃ pana, bhantai, saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā kiṃ sau pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘brahmaṃ, ānanda, puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, brahmaṃ puñ‍ña’’nti? ‘‘kappaṃ, ānanda, saggamhi maudatīti –

‘‘sukhā  saṅghassa sāmaggī, samaggānañ‍ca anuggahau|

samaggaratau dhammaṭṭhau, yaugakkhaimā na dhaṃsati|

saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvāna, kappaṃ saggamhi maudatī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

upālivaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

upāli ṭhapanā ubbāhau, upasampadanissayā|

sāmaṇairau ca dvai bhaidā, ānandaihi parai duvaiti||


5. ak‍kausavaggau

1. vivādasuttaṃ

41. atha  khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina saṅghai bhaṇḍanakalahaviggahavivādā uppaj‍janti, bhikkhū ca na phāsu phāsuṃ (?) viharantī’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti  dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| ayaṃ khau, upāli, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina saṅghai bhaṇḍanakalahaviggahavivādā uppaj‍janti, bhikkhū ca na phāsu viharantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamavivādamūlasuttaṃ

42. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, vivādamūlānī’’ti? ‘‘dasa khau, upāli, vivādamūlāni| katamāni dasa? idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti| imāni khau, upāli, dasa vivādamūlānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyavivādamūlasuttaṃ

43. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, vivādamūlānī’’ti? ‘‘dasa khau, upāli, vivādamūlāni| katamāni dasa? idhupāli, bhikkhū anāpattiṃ āpattīti dīpainti, āpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpainti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukāpattīti dīpainti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukāpattīti dīpainti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lāpattīti dīpainti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lāpattīti dīpainti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisāpattīti dīpainti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisāpattīti dīpainti, sappaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ appaṭikammāpattīti dīpainti, appaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ sappaṭikammāpattīti dīpainti| imāni khau, upāli, dasa vivādamūlānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kusinārasuttaṃ

44. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ viharati baliharaṇai vanasaṇḍai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

cūḷava॰ 399; pari॰ 436 ‘‘caudakaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca dhammā ajjhattaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhitabbā? caudakaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘parisuddhakāyasamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina| saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti parisuddhaina kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā kāyikaṃ sikkhassū’ti, itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘parisuddhavacīsamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi vacīsamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina| saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  parisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti parisuddhaina vacīsamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā vācasikaṃ sikkhassū’ti, itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittaṃ nu khau mai cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ| saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau  maittaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā sabrahmacārīsu maittaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaihī’ti, itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, caudakaina bhikkhunā  paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bahussutau nu khaumhi sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpā mai dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā āgamaṃ pariyāpuṇassū’ti, itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ubhayāni khau pana mai pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau| saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ubhayāni pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina  svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, ‘idaṃ panāyasmā, kattha vuttaṃ bhagavatā’ti, iti puṭṭhau na sampāyissati| tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā  vinayaṃ sikkhassū’ti, itissa bhavanti vattārau| imai pañ‍ca dhammā ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca  dhammā ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbā? ‘kālaina vakkhāmi, nau akālaina; bhūtaina vakkhāmi, nau abhūtaina; saṇhaina vakkhāmi, nau pharusaina; atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi, nau anatthasaṃhitaina; maittacittau vakkhāmi, nau dausantarau’ti – imai pañ‍ca dhammā ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbā| caudakaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. rājantaipurappavaisanasuttaṃ

45. pāci॰ 497 ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā rājantaipurappavaisanai| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā mahaisiyā saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti| tatra bhikkhu pavisati| mahaisī vā bhikkhuṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukarauti, bhikkhu vā mahaisiṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukarauti| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘addhā imaisaṃ kataṃ vā karissanti vā’ti! ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau añ‍ñataraṃ itthiṃ gantvā na sarati – ‘sā taina gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti’| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘na khau idha añ‍ñau  kauci pavisati, añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai añ‍ñataraṃ ratanaṃ nassati| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati, añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai abbhantarā guyhamantā bahiddhā sambhaidaṃ gacchanti| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati, añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai pitā vā puttaṃ patthaiti puttau vā pitaraṃ patthaiti| taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati, añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā nīcaṭṭhāniyaṃ uc‍cai ṭhānai ṭhapaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā uc‍caṭṭhāniyaṃ nīcai ṭhānai ṭhapaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā akālai sainaṃ uyyaujaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā kālai sainaṃ uyyaujaitvā antarāmaggatau nivattāpaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ  hauti – ‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu  khau pabbajitassa kamma’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, navamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ hatthisammaddaṃ assasammaddaṃ rathasammaddaṃ rajanīyāni rūpasaddagandharasaphauṭṭhabbāni, yāni na pabbajitassa sāruppāni| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dasamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa ādīnavā rājantaipurappavaisanai’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sak‍kasuttaṃ

46. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau sambahulā sak‍kā upāsakā tadahupausathai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau sak‍kai upāsakai bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘api nu tumhai, sak‍kā, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasathā’’ti? ‘‘appaikadā mayaṃ, bhantai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasāma, appaikadā na upavasāmā’’ti| ‘‘taisaṃ vau, sak‍kā, alābhā taisaṃ dul‍laddhaṃ, yai tumhai aivaṃ saukasabhayai jīvitai maraṇasabhayai jīvitai appaikadā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasatha, appaikadā na upavasatha|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, sak‍kā, idha purisau yaina kainaci kammaṭṭhānaina anāpaj‍ja akusalaṃ divasaṃ aḍḍhakahāpaṇaṃ nibbisaiyya| dakkhau  purisau uṭṭhānasampan‍nauti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, sak‍kā, idha purisau yaina kainaci kammaṭṭhānaina anāpaj‍ja akusalaṃ divasaṃ kahāpaṇaṃ nibbisaiyya| dakkhau purisau uṭṭhānasampan‍nauti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ, mañ‍ñatha, sak‍kā, idha purisau yaina kainaci kammaṭṭhānaina anāpaj‍ja akusalaṃ divasaṃ dvai kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya … tayau kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… cattārau kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… pañ‍ca kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… cha kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… satta kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… aṭṭha kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… nava kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… dasa kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… vīsa kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… tiṃsa kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… cattārīsaṃ kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… pañ‍ñāsaṃ kahāpaṇai nibbisaiyya… kahāpaṇasataṃ nibbisaiyya| dakkhau purisau uṭṭhānasampan‍nauti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, sak‍kā, api nu sau purisau divasai divasai kahāpaṇasataṃ kahāpaṇasahassaṃ  nibbisamānau laddhaṃ laddhaṃ nikkhipantau vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, sak‍kā, api nu sau purisau bhaugahaitu bhauganidānaṃ bhaugādhikaraṇaṃ aikaṃ vā rattiṃ aikaṃ vā divasaṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā rattiṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā divasaṃ aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘kāmā hi, bhantai, anic‍cā tucchā musā mausadhammā’’ti|

‘‘idha pana vau, sak‍kā, mama sāvakau dasa vassāni appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni satampi  vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya| sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| tiṭṭhantu, sak‍kā, dasa vassāni|

idha mama sāvakau nava vassāni… aṭṭha vassāni… satta vassāni… cha vassāni… pañ‍ca vassāni cattāri vassāni… tīṇi vassāni… dvai vassāni… aikaṃ vassaṃ appamattau ātāpī  pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni satampi vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya, sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī  vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| tiṭṭhatu, sak‍kā, aikaṃ vassaṃ|

idha mama sāvakau dasa māsai appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni satampi vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya, sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| tiṭṭhantu, sak‍kā, dasa māsā|

idha mama sāvakau nava māsai… aṭṭha māsai… satta māsai… cha māsai… pañ‍ca māsai… cattārau māsai… tayau māsai… dvai māsai… aikaṃ māsaṃ… aḍḍhamāsaṃ appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni  satampi vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya, sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| tiṭṭhatu, sak‍kā, aḍḍhamāsau|

idha mama sāvakau dasa rattindivai rattidivai (ka॰) appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni satampi vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya, sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| tiṭṭhantu, sak‍kā, dasa rattindivā|

idha mama sāvakau nava rattindivai… aṭṭha rattindivai… satta rattindivai… cha  rattindivai… pañ‍ca rattindivai… cattārau  rattindivai… tayau rattindivai… dvai rattindivai… aikaṃ rattindivaṃ appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau satampi vassāni satampi vassasatāni satampi vassasahassāni aikantasukhappaṭisaṃvaidī viharaiyya, sau ca khvassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sautāpan‍nau| taisaṃ vau, sak‍kā, alābhā taisaṃ dul‍laddhaṃ, yai tumhai aivaṃ saukasabhayai jīvitai maraṇasabhayai jīvitai appaikadā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasatha, appaikadā na upavasathā’’ti| ‘‘aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, aj‍jataggai aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasissāmā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahālisuttaṃ

47. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāli licchavi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahāli licchavi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai haitu, kau pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti? ‘‘laubhau khau, mahāli, haitu, laubhau pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā| dausau khau, mahāli, haitu, dausau pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā| mauhau khau, mahāli, haitu, mauhau pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā| ayaunisau manasikārau khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau  pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa  pavattiyā| micchāpaṇihitaṃ  khau, mahāli, cittaṃ haitu, micchāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyāti| ayaṃ khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti|

‘‘kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti? ‘‘alaubhau khau, mahāli, haitu, alaubhau pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| adausau khau, mahāli, haitu, adausau pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| amauhau khau, mahāli, haitu, amauhau pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| yaunisau manasikārau khau, mahāli, haitu, yaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| sammāpaṇihitaṃ khau, mahāli, cittaṃ haitu, sammāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| ayaṃ khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā| imai ca, mahāli, dasa dhammā laukai na saṃvij‍jaiyyuṃ, nayidha pañ‍ñāyaitha adhammacariyāvisamacariyāti vā dhammacariyāsamacariyāti vā| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, imai dasa dhammā laukai  saṃvij‍janti, tasmā pañ‍ñāyati adhammacariyāvisamacariyāti vā dhammacariyāsamacariyāti vā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. pabbajitaabhiṇhasuttaṃ

48. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā| katamai dasa? ‘vaivaṇṇiyamhi ajjhupagatau’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘parapaṭibaddhā mai jīvikā’ti pabbajitaina  abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘añ‍ñau mai ākappau karaṇīyau’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘kac‍ci nu khau mai attā sīlatau na upavadatī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘kac‍ci nu khau maṃ anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī sīlatau na upavadantī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘kammassakaumhi kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammapaṭisaraṇau, yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā  tassa dāyādau bhavissāmī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘kathaṃbhūtassa mai rattindivā vītivattantī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ suñ‍ñāgārai abhiramāmī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ; ‘atthi nu khau mai uttari manussadhammau alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau, yaināhaṃ yauhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), sauhaṃ (syā॰) pacchimai kālai sabrahmacārīhi puṭṭhau na maṅku bhavissāmī’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sarīraṭṭhadhammasuttaṃ

49. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sarīraṭṭhā| katamai dasa? sītaṃ, uṇhaṃ, jighacchā, pipāsā, uc‍cārau, passāvau, kāyasaṃvarau, vacīsaṃvarau, ājīvasaṃvarau, paunaubhavikau paunaubbhavikau (ka॰) bhavasaṅkhārau – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā sarīraṭṭhā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bhaṇḍanasuttaṃ

50. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā  bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā  upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharāmā’’ti| ‘‘na khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajitānaṃ, yaṃ tumhai bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharaiyyātha|

‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā piyakarāṇā garukarāṇā (?) saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati  sikkhāpadaisu, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā  manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni  – tattha dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrūpāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni  sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni – tattha dakkhau hauti analasau tatrūpāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ, ayampi dhammau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya  kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā  pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ak‍kausavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

vivādā  dvai ca mūlāni, kusinārapavaisanai|

sak‍kau mahāli abhiṇhaṃ, sarīraṭṭhā ca bhaṇḍanāti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. sacittavaggau

1. sacittasuttaṃ

51. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalau hauti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalau bhavissāmī’ti bhavissāmāti (syā॰) – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalau hauti? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udakapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati| nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaivattamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā bahukārā bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau bahukārau (ka॰) hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu – ‘abhijjhālu nu  khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anabhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, byāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, abyāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathinamiddhau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, ak‍kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusītau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhavīriyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, byāpan‍nacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhatau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikicchau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kaudhanau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusītau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau  taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anabhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, abyāpan‍nacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathinamiddhau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhatau bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikicchau bahulaṃ viharāmi, ak‍kaudhanau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhavīriyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisuyaiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sāriputtasuttaṃ

52. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘nau  cai, āvusau, bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalau hauti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalau bhavissāmī’ti – aivañhi vau, āvusau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalau hauti? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati| nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ  vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaivattamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti|

aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā bahukārā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu – ‘abhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ  viharāmi, anabhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, byāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, abyāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathinamiddhau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, ak‍kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusītau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhavīriyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti|

‘‘sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi…pai॰… asamāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya  adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau  ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai  panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anabhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi…pai॰… samāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisuyaiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ṭhitisuttaṃ

53. ‘‘ṭhitimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, na vaṇṇayāmi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pagaiva parihāniṃ| vuḍḍhiñ‍ca khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaṇṇayāmi kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhitiṃ nau hāniṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, hāni hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhiti nau vuḍḍhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yattakau hauti saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānaina, tassa tai dhammā naiva tiṭṭhanti nau vaḍḍhanti| hānimaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vadāmi kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhitiṃ nau vuḍḍhiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, hāni hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhiti nau vuḍḍhi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai ṭhiti hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau hāni nau vuḍḍhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yattakau hauti saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānaina, tassa tai dhammā naiva hāyanti nau vaḍḍhanti| ṭhitimaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vadāmi kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau hāniṃ nau vuḍḍhiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ṭhiti hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuḍḍhi nau hāni|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vuḍḍhi hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhiti nau hāni? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yattakau hauti saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānaina, tassa tai dhammā naiva tiṭṭhanti nau hāyanti| vuḍḍhimaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vadāmi kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhitiṃ nau hāniṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, vuḍḍhi hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ṭhiti nau hāni|

‘‘nau  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalau hauti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalau bhavissāmī’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalau hauti? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā  parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati| nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaivattamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā bahukārā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu – ‘abhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anabhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, byāpan‍nacittau nu  khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, abyāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathinamiddhau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, ak‍kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusītau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhavīriyau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhitau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, byāpan‍nacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhatau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikicchau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kaudhanau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusītau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya  adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca  vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anabhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, abyāpan‍nacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathinamiddhau bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhatau bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikicchau bahulaṃ viharāmi, ak‍kaudhanau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhavīriyau bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhitau bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisuyaiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samathasuttaṃ

54. ‘‘nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalau hauti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalau bhavissāmī’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalau hauti? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati| nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaivattamanau hauti  paripuṇṇasaṅkappau – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā bahukārā hauti  kusalaisu dhammaisu – ‘lābhī nu khaumhi ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na nu khaumhi lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, lābhī nu khaumhi adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, na nu khaumhi lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘lābhīmhi ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ajjhattaṃ caitausamathai patiṭṭhāya adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya yaugau karaṇīyau| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘sacai  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘lābhīmhi adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya patiṭṭhāya ajjhattaṃ caitausamathai yaugau karaṇīyau| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa|

‘‘sacai, pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina  bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau  ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘lābhīmhi ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisuyaiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau|

‘‘cīvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| piṇḍapātampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| saināsanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| gāmanigamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| janapadapadaisampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| puggalampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi|

‘‘‘cīvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau  panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā cīvaraṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ na saivitabbaṃ| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā cīvaraṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai  cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cīvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ  taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘piṇḍapātampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā piṇḍapātaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai piṇḍapātaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā  parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau piṇḍapātau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā piṇḍapātaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai piṇḍapātaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau piṇḍapātau saivitabbau| ‘piṇḍapātampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘saināsanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā saināsanaṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai saināsanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ na saivitabbaṃ| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā saināsanaṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai saināsanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘saināsanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘gāmanigamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā gāmanigamaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai gāmanigamaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau gāmanigamau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā gāmanigamaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai gāmanigamaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau gāmanigamau saivitabbau| ‘gāmanigamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘janapadapadaisampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā janapadapadaisaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai janapadapadaisaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā  dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau janapadapadaisau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā janapadapadaisaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai janapadapadaisaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau janapadapadaisau saivitabbau| ‘janapadapadaisampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘puggalampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau puggalau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau puggalau saivitabbau| ‘puggalampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. parihānasuttaṃ

55. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti bhikkhavauti (sī॰ syā॰)| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘‘parihānadhammau puggalau, parihānadhammau puggalau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| ‘aparihānadhammau puggalau, aparihānadhammau puggalau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, parihānadhammau puggalau vuttau bhagavatā, kittāvatā ca pana aparihānadhammau puggalau vuttau  bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘dūrataupi khau mayaṃ, āvusau, āgacchāma āyasmatau sāriputtassa santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| sādhu vatāyasmantaṃyaiva sāriputtaṃ paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| āyasmatau sāriputtassa sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘tainahāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, parihānadhammau puggalau vuttau bhagavatā? idhāvusau, bhikkhu assutañ‍caiva dhammaṃ na suṇāti, sutā cassa dhammā sammausaṃ gacchanti, yai cassa dhammā pubbai caitasau asamphuṭṭhapubbā tai cassa na samudācaranti, aviñ‍ñātañ‍caiva na vijānāti| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, parihānadhammau puggalau vuttau bhagavatā|

‘‘kittāvatā ca panāvusau, aparihānadhammau puggalau vuttau bhagavatā? idhāvusau, bhikkhu assutañ‍caiva dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutā cassa dhammā  na sammausaṃ gacchanti, yai cassa dhammā pubbai caitasau asamphuṭṭhapubbā tai cassa samudācaranti, aviñ‍ñātañ‍caiva vijānāti| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, aparihānadhammau puggalau vuttau bhagavatā|

‘‘nau cai, āvusau, bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalau hauti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalau bhavissāmī’ti – aivañhi vau, āvusau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalau hauti? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati| nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaivattamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau  – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā bahukārā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu – ‘anabhijjhālu nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, abyāpan‍nacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, vigatathinamiddhau nu khau bahulaṃ  viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, anuddhatau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, tiṇṇavicikicchau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, ak‍kaudhanau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati  nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu  nau, asaṃkiliṭṭhacittau nu khau bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, lābhī nu khaumhi ajjhattaṃ dhammapāmauj‍jassa, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, lābhī nu khaumhi ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau, lābhī nu khaumhi adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti|

‘‘sacai pana, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai kusalai dhammai attani na samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā sabbaisaṃyaiva imaisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, taina bhikkhunā sabbaisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aikac‍cai kusalai dhammai attani samanupassati, aikac‍cai kusalai dhammai attani  na samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā yai kusalai dhammai attani samanupassati taisu kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya, yai kusalai dhammai attani na samanupassati taisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau  ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā| tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, taina bhikkhunā yai kusalai dhammai attani samanupassati taisu kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya, yai kusalai dhammai attani na samanupassati taisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya  adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai kusalai dhammai attani samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā sabbaisvaiva imaisu kusalaisu  dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

56. ‘‘dasayimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā dasa? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, dasa sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ
