57. ‘‘dasayimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti  mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā dasa? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, puḷavakasañ‍ñā pulavakasañ‍ñā (sī॰ pī॰), puḷuvakasañ‍ñā (ka॰), a॰ ni॰ 1.463-472, vinīlakasañ‍ñā, vicchiddakasañ‍ñā, uddhumātakasañ‍ñā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, dasa sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. mūlakasuttaṃ

58. a॰ ni॰ 8.83 ‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃmūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamudayā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamausaraṇā sabbai dhammā, kiṃpamukhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃadhipataiyyā sabbai dhammā, kiṃuttarā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbai dhammā, kiṃaugadhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃpariyausānā sabbai dhammā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ kinti byākaraiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina  hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃmūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamudayā  sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamausaraṇā sabbai  dhammā kiṃpamukhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃ adhipataiyyā sabbai dhammā, kiṃuttarā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbai dhammā, kiṃaugadhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃpariyausānā sabbai dhammā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘chandamūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, manasikārasambhavā sabbai dhammā, phassasamudayā sabbai dhammā, vaidanāsamausaraṇā sabbai dhammā, samādhippamukhā sabbai dhammā, satādhipataiyyā sabbai dhammā, pañ‍ñuttarā sabbai dhammā, vimuttisārā sabbai dhammā, amataugadhā sabbai dhammā, nibbānapariyausānā sabbai dhammā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pabbaj‍jāsuttaṃ

59. ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yathāpabbaj‍jāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, na cuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassanti; anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, anattasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, asubhasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, ādīnavasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, laukassa samañ‍ca visamañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, laukassa bhavañ‍ca sambhavañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰) vibhavañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, laukassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, pahānasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca  nau cittaṃ bhavissati, virāgasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissati, niraudhasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca nau cittaṃ bhavissatī’ti – aivañhi  vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yathāpabbaj‍jāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti na cuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, anattasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, asubhasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, ādīnavasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, laukassa samañ‍ca visamañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, laukassa bhavañ‍ca vibhavañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, laukassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca ñatvā taṃsañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, pahānasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, virāgasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, niraudhasañ‍ñāparicitañ‍ca cittaṃ hauti, tassa dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. girimānandasuttaṃ

60. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā girimānandau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, girimānandau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaināyasmā girimānandau tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, ānanda, girimānandassa bhikkhunau dasa sañ‍ñā bhāsaiyyāsi, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ girimānandassa bhikkhunau dasa sañ‍ñā sutvā sau ābādhau ṭhānasau paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘katamā dasa? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, asubhasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā anabhiratisañ‍ñā (ka॰), sabbasaṅkhāraisu anicchāsañ‍ñā, ānāpānassati|

‘‘katamā cānanda, anic‍casañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’nti| iti imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anic‍cānupassī viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, anic‍casañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, anattasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā, sautaṃ anattā, saddā anattā, ghānaṃ anattā, gandhā anattā, jivhā anattā, rasā anattā, kāyā anattā, phauṭṭhabbā anattā, manau anattā, dhammā anattā’ti| iti imaisu chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu anattānupassī viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, anattasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā  cānanda, asubhasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānāppakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti| iti imasmiṃ kāyai asubhānupassī viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, asubhasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, ādīnavasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bahudukkhau khau ayaṃ  kāyau bahuādīnavau? iti imasmiṃ kāyai vividhā ābādhā uppaj‍janti, saiyyathidaṃ – cakkhuraugau sautaraugau ghānaraugau jivhāraugau kāyaraugau sīsaraugau kaṇṇaraugau mukharaugau dantaraugau auṭṭharaugau kāsau sāsau pināsau ḍāhau ḍahau (sī॰ syā॰) jarau kucchiraugau mucchā pakkhandikā sūlā visūcikā kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau sausau apamārau daddu kaṇḍu kacchu nakhasā vitacchikā lauhitaṃ pittaṃ lauhitapittaṃ (sī॰) madhumaihau aṃsā piḷakā bhagandalā pittasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā saimhasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā vātasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā san‍nipātikā ābādhā utupariṇāmajā ābādhā visamaparihārajā ābādhā aupak‍kamikā ābādhā kammavipākajā ābādhā sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā pipāsā uc‍cārau passāvau’ti| iti  imasmiṃ kāyai ādīnavānupassī viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ādīnavasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā  cānanda, pahānasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti, pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti, pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti, pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti, pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, pahānasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, virāgasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhippaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau nibbāna’nti| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, virāgasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, niraudhasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ  paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhippaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, niraudhasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu yai laukai upādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahantau viharati anupādiyantau| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anicchāsañ‍ñā? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasaṅkhāraisu aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, sabbasaṅkhāraisu  anicchāsañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, ānāpānassati? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati satauva passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti| dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti| rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti| rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ  passasāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ  passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… samādahaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… anic‍cānupassī…pai॰… virāgānupassī…pai॰… niraudhānupassī…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ānāpānassati|

‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, ānanda, girimānandassa bhikkhunau imā  dasa sañ‍ñā bhāsaiyyāsi, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ girimānandassa bhikkhunau imā dasa sañ‍ñā sutvā sau ābādhau ṭhānasau paṭippassambhaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau santikai imā dasa sañ‍ñā uggahaitvā yaināyasmā girimānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatau girimānandassa imā dasa sañ‍ñā abhāsi| atha khau āyasmatau girimānandassa dasa sañ‍ñā sutvā sau ābādhau ṭhānasau paṭippassambhi| vuṭṭhahi cāyasmā girimānandau tamhā ābādhā| tathā pahīnau ca panāyasmatau girimānandassa sau ābādhau ahausī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sacittavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sacittañ‍ca sāriputta, ṭhiti ca samathaina ca|

parihānau ca dvai sañ‍ñā, mūlā pabbajitaṃ girīti||


(7) 2. yamakavaggau

1. avij‍jāsuttaṃ

61. ‘‘purimā, bhikkhavai, kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāya – ‘itau pubbai avij‍jā nāhausi, atha pacchā samabhavī’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati, atha ca pana pañ‍ñāyati – ‘idappac‍cayā avij‍jā’ti|

‘‘avij‍jampāhaṃ avij‍jampahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau avij‍jāya? ‘pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| pañ‍capāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇai sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ? ‘tīṇi duc‍caritānī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| tīṇipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duc‍caritāni sāhārāni vadāmi, nau anāhārāni| kau cāhārau tiṇṇaṃ duc‍caritānaṃ? ‘indriyaasaṃvarau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| indriyaasaṃvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau indriyaasaṃvarassa? ‘asatāsampajañ‍ña’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| asatāsampajañ‍ñampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau asatāsampajañ‍ñassa? ‘ayaunisaumanasikārau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| ayaunisaumanasikārampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau ayaunisaumanasikārassa? ‘assaddhiya’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| assaddhiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau assaddhiyassa? ‘asaddhammassavana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| asaddhammassavanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau asaddhammassavanassa? ‘asappurisasaṃsaivau’tissa vacanīyaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau asaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, asaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ assaddhiyaṃ paripūraiti, assaddhiyaṃ paripūraṃ ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, ayaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyaasaṃvaraṃ  paripūraiti, indriyaasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi  duc‍caritāni paripūrāni pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai paripūrainti, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā paripūrā avij‍jaṃ paripūrainti| aivamaitissā avij‍jāya āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai  daivai vassantai ( ) (galagalāyantai) (sī॰), (gaḷagaḷāyantai) (syā॰) taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti, pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai kussubbhai (sī॰), kusubbhai (syā॰), kusaumbhai (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.96 paripūrainti| kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai mahāsaumbhai (ka॰) paripūrainti, mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti, kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti, mahānadiyau paripūrā mahāsamuddaṃ sāgaraṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitassa mahāsamuddassa sāgarassa āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau asaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, asaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ assaddhiyaṃ paripūraiti, assaddhiyaṃ paripūraṃ ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, ayaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyaasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyaasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūrāni pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai paripūrainti, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā paripūrā avij‍jaṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitissā avij‍jāya āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘vij‍jāvimuttimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau vij‍jāvimuttiyā? ‘satta baujjhaṅgā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| sattapāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgai sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ? ‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| cattāraupāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānai sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ? ‘tīṇi sucaritānī’tissa  vacanīyaṃ| tīṇipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sucaritāni sāhārāni vadāmi, nau anāhārāni| kau cāhārau tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ? ‘indriyasaṃvarau’tissa  vacanīyaṃ| indriyasaṃvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau indriyasaṃvarassa? ‘satisampajañ‍ña’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| satisampajañ‍ñampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau satisampajañ‍ñassa? ‘yaunisaumanasikārau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| yaunisaumanasikārampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau yaunisaumanasikārassa? ‘saddhā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| saddhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau saddhāya? ‘saddhammassavana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| saddhammassavanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau saddhammassavanassa? ‘sappurisasaṃsaivau’tissa vacanīyaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ saddhaṃ paripūraiti, saddhā paripūrā yaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, yaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau satisampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, satisampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi sucaritāni paripūrāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūrainti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā paripūrā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā paripūrā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitissā vij‍jāvimuttiyā āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti, pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai paripūrainti, kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai  paripūrainti, mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti, kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti, mahānadiyau paripūrā mahāsamuddaṃ  sāgaraṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitassa mahāsamuddassa sāgarassa āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ saddhaṃ paripūraiti, saddhā paripūrā yaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, yaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau satisampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, satisampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi sucaritāni paripūrāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūrainti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā paripūrā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā paripūrā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitissā vij‍jāvimuttiyā āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūrī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. taṇhāsuttaṃ

62. ‘‘purimā, bhikkhavai, kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati bhavataṇhāya – ‘itau pubbai bhavataṇhā nāhausi, atha pacchā samabhavī’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati, atha ca pana pañ‍ñāyati – ‘idappac‍cayā bhavataṇhā’ti|

‘‘bhavataṇhāmpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau bhavataṇhāya? ‘avij‍jā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| avij‍jampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau avij‍jāya? ‘pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| pañ‍ca nīvaraṇaipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ? ‘tīṇi duc‍caritānī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| tīṇipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duc‍caritāni sāhārāni vadāmi, nau anāhārāni| kau cāhārau tiṇṇan‍naṃ duc‍caritānaṃ? ‘indriyaasaṃvarau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| indriyaasaṃvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau indriyaasaṃvarassa? ‘asatāsampajañ‍ña’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| asatāsampajañ‍ñampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau asatā sampajañ‍ñassa? ‘ayaunisaumanasikārau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| ayaunisaumanasikārampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau ayaunisaumanasikārassa? ‘assaddhiya’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| assaddhiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau assaddhiyassa? ‘assaddhammassavana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| assaddhammassavanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau assaddhammassavanassa? ‘asappurisasaṃsaivau’tissa vacanīyaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau assaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, assaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ assaddhiyaṃ paripūraiti, assaddhiyaṃ paripūraṃ ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, ayaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyaasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyaasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūrāni pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai paripūrainti, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā paripūrā avij‍jaṃ paripūrainti, avij‍jā paripūrā bhavataṇhaṃ paripūraiti; aivamaitissā bhavataṇhāya āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ  pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti, pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai paripūrainti, kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai paripūrainti, mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti, kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti, mahānadiyau paripūrā mahāsamuddaṃ  sāgaraṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitassa mahāsamuddassa sāgarassa āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau assaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, assaddhammassavanaṃ paripūraṃ assaddhiyaṃ  paripūraiti, assaddhiyaṃ paripūraṃ ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, ayaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, asatāsampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraṃ indriyaasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyaasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi duc‍caritāni paripūrāni pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai paripūrainti, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā paripūrā avij‍jaṃ paripūrainti, avij‍jā paripūrā bhavataṇhaṃ paripūraiti; aivamaitissā bhavataṇhāya āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘vij‍jāvimuttimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau vij‍jāvimuttiyā? ‘satta baujjhaṅgā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| sattapāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgai sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ? ‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| cattāraupāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānai sāhārai vadāmi, nau anāhārai| kau cāhārau catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ? ‘tīṇi sucaritānī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| tīṇipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sucaritāni sāhārāni vadāmi, nau anāhārāni| kau cāhārau tiṇṇan‍naṃ  sucaritānaṃ? ‘indriyasaṃvarau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| indriyasaṃvarampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau indriyasaṃvarassa? ‘satisampajañ‍ña’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| satisampajañ‍ñampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau satisampajañ‍ñassa? ‘yaunisaumanasikārau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| yaunisaumanasikārampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau yaunisaumanasikārassa? ‘saddhā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| saddhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau saddhāya? ‘saddhammassavana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| saddhammassavanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāhāraṃ vadāmi, nau anāhāraṃ| kau cāhārau saddhammassavanassa? ‘sappurisasaṃsaivau’tissa vacanīyaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti, saddhammassavanaṃ  paripūraṃ saddhaṃ paripūraiti, saddhā paripūrā yaunisaumanasikāraṃ paripūraiti, yaunisaumanasikārau paripūrau satisampajañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, satisampajañ‍ñaṃ  paripūraṃ indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyasaṃvarau paripūrau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraiti, tīṇi sucaritāni paripūrāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūrainti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā paripūrā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā paripūrā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti; aivamaitissā vij‍jāvimuttiyā āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa mahāsamuddassa sāgarassa āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūri| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisasaṃsaivau paripūrau saddhammassavanaṃ paripūraiti…pai॰…  aivamaitissā vij‍jāvimuttiyā āhārau hauti, aivañ‍ca pāripūrī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. niṭṭhaṅgatasuttaṃ

63. ‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi niṭṭhaṃ gatā sabbai tai diṭṭhisampan‍nā| taisaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā? sattakkhattuparamassa, kaulaṃkaulassa, aikabījissa, sakadāgāmissa, yau ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai arahā – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā? antarāparinibbāyissa, upahac‍caparinibbāyissa, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, uddhaṃsautassa akaniṭṭhagāminau – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi niṭṭhaṃ gatā, sabbai tai diṭṭhisampan‍nā| taisaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. avaic‍cappasan‍nasuttaṃ

64. ‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi avaic‍cappasan‍nā, sabbai tai sautāpan‍nā| taisaṃ sautāpan‍nānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā? sattakkhattuparamassa, kaulaṃkaulassa, aikabījissa, sakadāgāmissa, yau ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai arahā  – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā? antarāparinibbāyissa, upahac‍caparinibbāyissa, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, uddhaṃsautassa akaniṭṭhagāminau – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi avaic‍cappasan‍nā sabbai tai sautāpan‍nā| taisaṃ  sautāpan‍nānaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasukhasuttaṃ

65. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau magadhaisu viharati nālakagāmakai| atha khau sāmaṇḍakāni paribbājakau yaināyasmā  sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sāmaṇḍakāni paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, sukhaṃ, kiṃ dukkha’’nti? ‘‘abhinibbatti khau, āvusau, dukkhā, anabhinibbatti sukhā| abhinibbattiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ dukkhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā pipāsā uc‍cārau passāvau aggisamphassau daṇḍasamphassau satthasamphassau ñātīpi mittāpi saṅgamma samāgamma rausainti| abhinibbattiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ dukkhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ| anabhinibbattiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ sukhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – na sītaṃ na uṇhaṃ na jighacchā na pipāsā na uc‍cārau na passāvau na aggisamphassau na daṇḍasamphassau na satthasamphassau ñātīpi mittāpi saṅgamma samāgamma na rausainti| anabhinibbattiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ sukhaṃ pāṭikaṅkha’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasukhasuttaṃ

66. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau magadhaisu viharati nālakagāmakai| atha khau sāmaṇḍakāni paribbājakau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sāmaṇḍakāni paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ  sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, āvusau, sāriputta, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai sukhaṃ, kiṃ dukkha’’nti? ‘‘anabhirati khau, āvusau, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai dukkhā, abhirati sukhā| anabhiratiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ dukkhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – gacchantaupi sukhaṃ sātaṃ  nādhigacchati, ṭhitaupi… nisin‍naupi… sayānaupi… gāmagataupi… arañ‍ñagataupi… rukkhamūlagataupi… suñ‍ñāgāragataupi… abbhaukāsagataupi… bhikkhumajjhagataupi sukhaṃ sātaṃ nādhigacchati| anabhiratiyā, āvusau, sati  idaṃ dukkhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ|

‘‘abhiratiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ sukhaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – gacchantaupi sukhaṃ sātaṃ adhigacchati, ṭhitaupi… nisin‍naupi… sayānaupi… gāmagataupi… arañ‍ñagataupi… rukkhamūlagataupi… suñ‍ñāgāragataupi… abbhaukāsagataupi… bhikkhumajjhagataupi sukhaṃ sātaṃ adhigacchati| abhiratiyā, āvusau, sati idaṃ sukhaṃ pāṭikaṅkha’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamanaḷakapānasuttaṃ

67. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina naḷakapānaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ nigamau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā naḷakapānai viharati palāsavanai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi –

‘‘vigatathinamiddhau vigatathīnamiddhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khau, sāriputta, bhikkhusaṅghau| paṭibhātu taṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammī kathā| piṭṭhi mai āgilāyati; tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau  bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘yassa  kassaci, āvusau, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī hiri (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) natthi… auttappaṃ natthi … vīriyaṃ viriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina hāyati maṇḍalaina hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘assaddhau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘ahirikau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘anauttappī purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘kusītau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘duppañ‍ñau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘kaudhanau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘upanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘pāpicchau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘pāpamittau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘micchādiṭṭhikau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, parihānamaitaṃ|

‘‘yassa kassaci, āvusau, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā  atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina  vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni|

‘‘‘saddhau  purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘hirīmā purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘auttappī purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘āraddhavīriyau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘pañ‍ñavā purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘ak‍kaudhanau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘anupanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘appicchau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘kalyāṇamittau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘sammādiṭṭhikau purisapuggalau’ti, āvusau, aparihānamaita’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā pac‍cuṭṭhāya āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yassa kassaci, sāriputta, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina hāyati maṇḍalaina hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāriputta, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu  dhammaisu…pai॰… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā …pai॰… nau vuddhi|

‘‘‘assaddhau purisapuggalau’ti, sāriputta, parihānamaitaṃ; ahirikau… anauttappī… kusītau… duppañ‍ñau… kaudhanau… upanāhī… pāpicchau… pāpamittau… ‘micchādiṭṭhikau purisapuggalau’ti, sāriputta, parihānamaitaṃ|

‘‘yassa kassaci, sāriputta, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāriputta, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu  dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni|

‘‘‘saddhau  purisapuggalau’ti, sāriputta, aparihānamaitaṃ; hirīmā… auttappī… āraddhavīriyau… pañ‍ñavā… ak‍kaudhanau… anupanāhī… appicchau… kalyāṇamittau… ‘sammādiṭṭhikau purisapuggalau’ti, sāriputta, aparihānamaita’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyanaḷakapānasuttaṃ

68. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā naḷakapānai viharati palāsavanai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā  samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi –

‘‘vigatathinamiddhau khau, sāriputta, bhikkhusaṅghau| paṭibhātu taṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhūnaṃ  dhammī kathā| piṭṭhi mai āgilāyati; tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavai’’ti! ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘yassa kassaci, āvusau, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi… sautāvadhānaṃ natthi… dhammadhāraṇā natthi… atthūpaparikkhā natthi… dhammānudhammappaṭipatti natthi… appamādau natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina  hāyati maṇḍalaina hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi… sautāvadhānaṃ natthi… dhammadhāraṇā natthi… atthūpaparikkhā natthi… dhammānudhammappaṭipatti  natthi… appamādau natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā  divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘yassa kassaci, āvusau, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi… sautāvadhānaṃ atthi… dhammadhāraṇā atthi… atthūpaparikkhā atthi… dhammānudhammappaṭipatti atthi… appamādau atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, juṇhapakkhai  candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… appamādau atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihānī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā pac‍cuṭṭhāya āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yassa kassaci, sāriputta, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… sautāvadhānaṃ natthi… dhammadhāraṇā natthi… atthūpaparikkhā natthi… dhammānudhammappaṭipatti natthi… appamādau natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina hāyati maṇḍalaina hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāriputta, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… appamādau  natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau vuddhi|

‘‘yassa kassaci, sāriputta, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi… sautāvadhānaṃ atthi… dhammadhāraṇā atthi… atthūpaparikkhā  atthi… dhammānudhammappaṭipatti atthi… appamādau atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihāni| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina  vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati  ārauhapariṇāhaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāriputta, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… appamādau atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamakathāvatthusuttaṃ

69. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā  upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – dī॰ ni॰ 1.17; ma॰ ni॰ 2.223; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1080; pāci॰ 508 rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.223 passitabbaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vāti|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharāma, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā’’ti| ‘‘na khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ  saddhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ, yaṃ tumhai anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharaiyyātha, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ  nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vāti|

‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, kathāvatthūni| katamāni dasa? appicchakathā, santuṭṭhikathā, pavivaikakathā, asaṃsaggakathā, vīriyārambhakathā, sīlakathā, samādhikathā, pañ‍ñākathā, vimuttikathā, vimuttiñāṇadassanakathāti – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa kathāvatthūni|

‘‘imaisaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ kathāvatthūnaṃ upādāyupādāya kathaṃ kathaiyyātha, imaisampi candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃmahānubhāvānaṃ taijasā taijaṃ pariyādiyaiyyātha, kau pana vādau añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakāna’’nti! navamaṃ|

10. dutiyakathāvatthusuttaṃ

70. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ  iti vāti|

‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, pāsaṃsāni ṭhānāni| katamāni dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā ca appicchau hauti, appicchakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘appicchau bhikkhu appicchakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca santuṭṭhau hauti, santuṭṭhikathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘santuṭṭhau bhikkhu santuṭṭhikathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca pavivittau hauti, pavivaikakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘pavivittau bhikkhu pavivaikakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā  ca asaṃsaṭṭhau hauti, asaṃsaṭṭhakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘asaṃsaṭṭhau bhikkhu asaṃsaṭṭhakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca āraddhavīriyau hauti, vīriyārambhakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘āraddhavīriyau bhikkhu vīriyārambhakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā  ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘sīlasampan‍nau bhikkhu sīlasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘samādhisampan‍nau bhikkhu samādhisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau bhikkhu pañ‍ñāsampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā hauti| ‘vimuttisampan‍nau bhikkhu vimuttisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

‘‘attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ  kattā hauti| ‘vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau bhikkhu vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā’ti pāsaṃsamaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa pāsaṃsāni ṭhānānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

yamakavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

avij‍jā  taṇhā niṭṭhā ca, avaic‍ca dvai sukhāni ca|

naḷakapānai dvai vuttā, kathāvatthūparai duvaiti||


(8) 3. ākaṅkhavaggau

1. ākaṅkhasuttaṃ

71. a॰ ni॰ 4.12; itivu॰ 111 aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sampan‍nasīlā, bhikkhavai, viharatha sampan‍napātimaukkhā, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutā viharatha ācāragaucarasampan‍nā aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvinau, samādāya sikkhatha sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau cassaṃ manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’ti, sīlaisvaivassa paripūrakārī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau  anirākatajjhānau vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘lābhī assaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’nti, sīlaisvaivassa paripūrakārī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘yaisāhaṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ taisaṃ tai kārā mahapphalā assu mahānisaṃsā’ti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘yai mai yai maṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 1.65) paitā ñātī sālauhitā kālaṅkatā kālakatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pasan‍nacittā anussaranti taisaṃ taṃ mahapphalaṃ assa mahānisaṃsa’nti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘santuṭṭhau assaṃ itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārainā’ti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘khamau assaṃ sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ, duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ tippānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau assa’nti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘aratiratisahau assaṃ, na ca maṃ aratirati sahaiyya, uppan‍naṃ aratiratiṃ abhibhuyya  abhibhuyya viharaiyya’nti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘bhayabhairavasahau assaṃ, na ca maṃ bhayabhairavau sahaiyya, uppan‍naṃ bhayabhairavaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya’nti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī assaṃ akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’ti, sīlaisvaivassa…pai॰… brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, sīlaisvaivassa paripūrakārī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ|

‘‘‘sampan‍nasīlā, bhikkhavai, viharatha sampan‍napātimaukkhā, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutā viharatha ācāragaucarasampan‍nā aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvinau, samādāya sikkhatha sikkhāpadaisū’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaṇṭakasuttaṃ

72. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ sambahulaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ – āyasmatā ca cālaina pālaina (syā॰), āyasmatā ca upacālaina uppālaina (syā॰), āyasmatā ca kuk‍kuṭaina kak‍kaṭaina (sī॰ syā॰), āyasmatā ca kaḷimbhaina kavimbhaina (sī॰), āyasmatā ca nikaṭaina kaṭaina (sī॰), āyasmatā ca kaṭissahaina; añ‍ñaihi ca abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī bhadraihi  bhadraihi yānaihi parapurāya paraṃpurāya (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhanti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| atha khau taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi parapurāya uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhanti  bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| ‘saddakaṇṭakā khau pana jhānā’ vuttā bhagavatā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina gausiṅgasālavanadāyau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma| tattha mayaṃ appasaddā appākiṇṇā phāsuṃ phāsu (syā॰ ka॰) viharaiyyāmā’’ti| atha khau tai āyasmantau yaina gausiṅgasālavanadāyau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; tattha tai āyasmantau appasaddā appākiṇṇā phāsuṃ viharanti|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, cālau, kahaṃ upacālau, kahaṃ kuk‍kuṭau, kahaṃ kaḷimbhau, kahaṃ nikaṭau, kahaṃ kaṭissahau; kahaṃ nu khau tai, bhikkhavai, thairā sāvakā gatā’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhantai, taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘imai khau sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi parapurāya uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhanti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya ‘saddakaṇṭakā khau pana jhānāvuttā bhagavatā yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina gausiṅgasālavanadāyau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma tattha mayaṃ appasaddā appākiṇṇā phāsuṃ viharaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau tai, bhantai, āyasmantau yaina gausiṅgasālavanadāyau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| tattha tai āyasmantau appasaddā appākiṇṇā phāsuṃ viharantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, bhikkhavai, yathā tai mahāsāvakā sammā byākaramānā byākaraiyyuṃ, ‘saddakaṇṭakā hi, bhikkhavai, jhānā’ vuttā mayā|

‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, kaṇṭakā| katamai dasa? pavivaikārāmassa saṅgaṇikārāmatā kaṇṭakau, asubhanimittānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa subhanimittānuyaugau kaṇṭakau, indriyaisu guttadvārassa visūkadassanaṃ kaṇṭakau, brahmacariyassa mātugāmūpacārau mātugāmaupavicārau (sī॰), mātugāmūpavicarau (ka॰) kaṇṭakau, kathā॰ 333 paṭhamassa  jhānassa saddau kaṇṭakau, dutiyassa jhānassa vitak‍kavicārā kaṇṭakā, tatiyassa jhānassa pīti kaṇṭakau, catutthassa jhānassa assāsapassāsau kaṇṭakau, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca kaṇṭakau rāgau kaṇṭakau dausau kaṇṭakau mauhau kaṇṭakau|

‘‘akaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, viharatha| nik‍kaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, viharatha| akaṇṭakanik‍kaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, viharatha| akaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, arahantau; nik‍kaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, arahantau; akaṇṭakanik‍kaṇṭakā, bhikkhavai, arahantau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. iṭṭhadhammasuttaṃ

73. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ| katamai dasa? bhaugā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ; vaṇṇau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ; āraugyaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ dul‍labhaṃ laukasmiṃ; sīlaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ dul‍labhaṃ laukasmiṃ; brahmacariyaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ dul‍labhaṃ laukasmiṃ; mittā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ; bāhusac‍caṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ dul‍labhaṃ laukasmiṃ; pañ‍ñā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ; dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ; saggā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ dasa dhammā paripanthā paribandhā (ka॰) – ālasyaṃ anuṭṭhānaṃ bhaugānaṃ paripanthau, amaṇḍanā avibhūsanā vaṇṇassa paripanthau, asappāyakiriyā āraugyassa paripanthau, pāpamittatā sīlānaṃ paripanthau, indriyaasaṃvarau brahmacariyassa paripanthau, visaṃvādanā mittānaṃ paripanthau, asajjhāyakiriyā bāhusac‍cassa  paripanthau, asussūsā aparipucchā pañ‍ñāya paripanthau, ananuyaugau apac‍cavaikkhaṇā dhammānaṃ paripanthau, micchāpaṭipatti saggānaṃ paripanthau| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ imai dasa dhammā paripanthā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ dasa dhammā āhārā – uṭṭhānaṃ anālasyaṃ bhaugānaṃ āhārau, maṇḍanā vibhūsanā vaṇṇassa āhārau, sappāyakiriyā āraugyassa āhārau, kalyāṇamittatā sīlānaṃ āhārau, indriyasaṃvarau brahmacariyassa āhārau, avisaṃvādanā mittānaṃ āhārau, sajjhāyakiriyā bāhusac‍cassa āhārau, sussūsā paripucchā pañ‍ñāya āhārau, anuyaugau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā dhammānaṃ āhārau, sammāpaṭipatti saggānaṃ āhārau| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ imai dasa dhammā āhārā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vaḍḍhisuttaṃ

74. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānau ariyasāvakau ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyī ca hauti varādāyī kāyassa| katamaihi dasahi? khaittavatthūhi vaḍḍhati, dhanadhañ‍ñaina vaḍḍhati, puttadāraihi vaḍḍhati, dāsakammakarapaurisaihi vaḍḍhati, catuppadaihi vaḍḍhati, saddhāya vaḍḍhati, sīlaina vaḍḍhati, sutaina vaḍḍhati, cāgaina vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānau ariyasāvakau ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyī ca hauti varādāyī kāyassāti|

‘‘dhanaina dhañ‍ñaina ca yaudha vaḍḍhati,

puttaihi dāraihi catuppadaihi ca|

sa bhaugavā hauti yasassi pūjitau,

ñātīhi mittaihi athaupi rājubhi||

‘‘saddhāya sīlaina ca yaudha vaḍḍhati,

pañ‍ñāya cāgaina sutaina cūbhayaṃ|

sau  tādisau sappurisau vicakkhaṇau,

diṭṭhaiva dhammai ubhayaina vaḍḍhatī’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. migasālāsuttaṃ

75. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau migasālā upāsikā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā  khau migasālā upāsikā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau  daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāyaṃ| pitā mai, bhantai, purāṇau brahmacārī hauti  ārācārī anācārī (ka॰) viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| sau kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sakadāgāmī sattau sakadāgāmisattau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau’ti| pitāmahau mai paittāpi yau mai (sī॰), pita piyau mai (syā॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.44, bhantai, isidattau abrahmacārī ahausi sadārasantuṭṭhau| saupi kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sakadāgāmī sattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhagini, bhagavatā byākata’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, migasālā upāsikā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau, bhantai, migasālā upāsikā maṃ aitadavauca  –

‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau  añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāyaṃ| pitā mai, bhantai, purāṇau brahmacārī ahausi ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| sau kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau sakadāgāmī sattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti| pitāmahau mai, bhantai, isidattau abrahmacārī ahausi sadārasantuṭṭhau| saupi kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau – sakadāgāmī sattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti|

kathaṃ  kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāya’nti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, migasālaṃ upāsikaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhagini, bhagavatā byākata’’’nti|

‘‘kā cānanda, migasālā upāsikā bālā abyattā ammakā ammakapañ‍ñā ambakā ambakapañ‍ñā (sī॰ pī॰), andhakā andhakapañ‍ñā (syā॰), kai ca purisapuggalaparaupariyai ñāṇai?

‘‘dasayimai, ānanda, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai dasa? idhānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa taṃ dussīlyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti, nau visaisāya; hānagāmīyaiva hauti, nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti yatthassa  taṃ dussīlyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ (syā॰) hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti, nau hānāya; visaisagāmīyaiva hauti, nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti – ‘imassapi taiva dhammā, aparassapi taiva dhammā| kasmā naisaṃ aikau hīnau aikau paṇītau’ti? tañhi taisaṃ, ānanda, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau dussīlau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa taṃ dussīlyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, puggalau amunā purimaina puggalaina abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imaṃ hānanda, puggalaṃ dhammasautau nibbahati| tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgataina! tasmātihānanda, mā puggalaisu pamāṇikā ahuvattha, mā puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha| khañ‍ñati hānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhantau| ahaṃ vā, ānanda ahañ‍cānanda (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.44 passitabbaṃ, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha  panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa taṃ sīlaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ  na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti, nau visaisāya; hānagāmīyaiva hauti, nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa taṃ sīlaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti, nau hānāya; visaisagāmīyaiva hauti, nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti…pai॰… ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau tibbarāgau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa sau rāgau aparisaisau nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti, nau visaisāya; hānagāmīyaiva hauti, nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau tibbarāgau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa sau rāgau aparisaisau nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti, nau hānāya; visaisagāmīyaiva hauti, nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti…pai॰… ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ yau vā  panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau kaudhanau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa sau kaudhau aparisaisau nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti, nau visaisāya; hānagāmīyaiva hauti, nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau kaudhanau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti yatthassa sau kaudhau aparisaisau nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti, nau hānāya; visaisagāmīyaiva hauti, nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti…pai॰… ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau uddhatau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa taṃ uddhac‍caṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti, nau visaisāya; hānagāmīyaiva hauti, nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau uddhatau hauti| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa taṃ uddhac‍caṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| tassa savanainapi  kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi  paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti, nau hānāya; visaisagāmīyaiva hauti, nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti – ‘imassapi taiva dhammā, aparassapi taiva dhammā| kasmā naisaṃ aikau hīnau aikau paṇītau’ti? tañhi taisaṃ, ānanda, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau uddhatau hauti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa taṃ uddhac‍caṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati, tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, puggalau amunā purimaina puggalaina abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imaṃ hānanda, puggalaṃ dhammasautau nibbahati| tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgataina! tasmātihānanda, mā puggalaisu pamāṇikā ahuvattha; mā puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha| khañ‍ñati hānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhantau| ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘kā cānanda, migasālā upāsikā bālā abyattā ammakā ammakapañ‍ñā, kai ca purisapuggalaparaupariyai ñāṇai! imai khau, ānanda, dasa puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘yathārūpaina, ānanda, sīlaina purāṇau saman‍nāgatau ahausi tathārūpaina sīlaina isidattau saman‍nāgatau abhavissa, nayidha purāṇau isidattassa  gatimpi añ‍ñassa| yathārūpāya  cānanda, pañ‍ñāya isidattau saman‍nāgatau ahausi tathārūpāya pañ‍ñāya purāṇau saman‍nāgatau abhavissa, nayidha isidattau purāṇassa gatimpi añ‍ñassa| iti khau, ānanda, imai puggalā ubhau aikaṅgahīnā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tayaudhammasuttaṃ

76. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā laukai na saṃvij‍jaiyyuṃ, na tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jaiyya arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, na tathāgatappavaiditau dhammavinayau laukai dibbaiyya| katamai tayau? jāti ca, jarā ca, maraṇañ‍ca – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā laukai na saṃvij‍jaiyyuṃ, na tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jaiyya arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, na tathāgatappavaiditau dhammavinayau laukai dibbaiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, imai tayau dhammā laukai saṃvij‍janti tasmā tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, tasmā tathāgatappavaiditau dhammavinayau laukai dibbati|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? rāgaṃ appahāya, dausaṃ appahāya, mauhaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai  tayau? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ appahāya, vicikicchaṃ appahāya, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ  pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ appahāya, kummaggasaivanaṃ appahāya, caitasau līnattaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau ayaunisau manasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? muṭṭhasac‍caṃ appahāya, asampajañ‍ñaṃ appahāya, caitasau vikkhaipaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau muṭṭhasac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ appahāya, ariyadhammassa ariyadhammaṃ (syā॰) asautukamyataṃ appahāya, upārambhacittataṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau muṭṭhasac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? uddhac‍caṃ appahāya, asaṃvaraṃ appahāya, dussīlyaṃ appahāya  – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? assaddhiyaṃ appahāya, avadañ‍ñutaṃ appahāya, kausaj‍jaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? anādariyaṃ appahāya, dauvacassataṃ appahāya, pāpamittataṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ahirikaṃ appahāya, anauttappaṃ appahāya, pamādaṃ appahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai appahāya abhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘ahirikauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, anauttāpī pamattau hauti| sau pamattau samānau abhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ| sau pāpamittau samānau abhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pahātuṃ| sau kusītau samānau abhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ| sau dussīlau samānau abhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ| sau upārambhacittau samānau abhabbau muṭṭhasac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ| sau vikkhittacittau  samānau abhabbau ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ| sau līnacittau samānau  abhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ| sau vicikicchau samānau abhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ  pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ| sau rāgaṃ appahāya dausaṃ appahāya mauhaṃ appahāya abhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? rāgaṃ pahāya, dausaṃ pahāya, mauhaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahāya, vicikicchaṃ pahāya, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahāya, kummaggasaivanaṃ pahāya, caitasau līnattaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? muṭṭhasac‍caṃ pahāya, asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahāya, caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ  caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau muṭṭhasac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahāya, ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahāya, upārambhacittataṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau muṭṭhassac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? uddhac‍caṃ pahāya, asaṃvaraṃ pahāya, dussīlyaṃ pahāya – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? assaddhiyaṃ pahāya, avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahāya, kausaj‍jaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ  pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? anādariyaṃ pahāya, dauvacassataṃ pahāya, pāpamittataṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai  pahāya bhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ| katamai tayau? ahirikaṃ pahāya, anauttappaṃ pahāya, pamādaṃ pahāya – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammai pahāya bhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ|

‘‘hirīmāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, auttāpī appamattau hauti| sau appamattau samānau bhabbau anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dauvacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ| sau kalyāṇamittau samānau  bhabbau assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadañ‍ñutaṃ pahātuṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pahātuṃ| sau āraddhavīriyau samānau bhabbau uddhac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ| sau sīlavā samānau bhabbau ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammassa asautukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ| sau anupārambhacittau samānau bhabbau muṭṭhassac‍caṃ pahātuṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau vikkhaipaṃ pahātuṃ| sau avikkhittacittau samānau bhabbau ayaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasaivanaṃ pahātuṃ caitasau līnattaṃ pahātuṃ| sau alīnacittau samānau bhabbau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ| sau avicikicchau samānau bhabbau rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dausaṃ pahātuṃ mauhaṃ pahātuṃ| sau rāgaṃ pahāya  dausaṃ pahāya mauhaṃ pahāya bhabbau jātiṃ pahātuṃ jaraṃ pahātuṃ maraṇaṃ pahātu’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kākasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kākau| katamaihi dasahi? dhaṃsī ca, pagabbhau ca, tintiṇau nil‍laj‍jau (ka॰) tintiṇauti tintiṇaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā… (sī॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰) abhidhammai khuddakavatthuvibhaṅgai tintiṇapadaniddaisai passitabbaṃ ca, mahagghasau ca, luddau ca, akāruṇikau ca, dubbalau ca, auravitā ca, muṭṭhassati ca, naicayikau nairasikau (sī॰) tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘naicayikau’’ tvaiva dissati ca – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kākau| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhu| katamaihi dasahi? dhaṃsī ca, pagabbhau ca, tintiṇau ca, mahagghasau ca, luddau ca, akāruṇikau  ca, dubbalau ca, auravitā ca, muṭṭhassati ca, naicayikau ca – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhū’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nigaṇṭhasuttaṃ

78. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatā nigaṇṭhā| katamaihi dasahi? assaddhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; dussīlā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; ahirikā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; anauttappinau, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; asappurisasambhattinau, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; attuk‍kaṃsakaparavambhakā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; sandiṭṭhiparāmāsā ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissagginau, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; kuhakā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; pāpicchā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā; pāpamittā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi asaddhammaihi saman‍nāgatā nigaṇṭhā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. āghātavatthusuttaṃ

79. a॰ ni॰ 9.29 ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, āghātavatthūni| katamāni dasa? ‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acarī’ti…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ caratī’ti…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acarī’ti…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ caratī’ti…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ carissatī’ti  āghātaṃ bandhati; aṭṭhānai ca kuppati – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa āghātavatthūnī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āghātapaṭivinayasuttaṃ

80. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, āghātapaṭivinayā| katamai dasa? ‘anatthaṃ mai acari, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti, ‘anatthaṃ mai carati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti, ‘anatthaṃ mai carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti, piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acari…pai॰… carati…pai॰… carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhāti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti, appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acari…pai॰… atthaṃ carati…pai॰… atthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhāti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti, aṭṭhānai ca na kuppati – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa āghātapaṭivinayā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ākaṅkhavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ākaṅkhau kaṇṭakau iṭṭhā, vaḍḍhi ca migasālāya|

tayau dhammā ca kākau ca, nigaṇṭhā dvai ca āghātāti||


(9) 4. thairavaggau

1. vāhanasuttaṃ

81. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| atha khau āyasmā vāhanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā vāhanau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi tathāgatau nissaṭau visaṃyuttau vippamuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā  viharatī’’ti?

‘‘dasahi  khau, vāhana, dhammaihi tathāgatau nissaṭau visaṃyuttau vippamuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| katamaihi dasahi? rūpaina khau, vāhana, tathāgatau nissaṭau visaṃyuttau vippamuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā  viharati, vaidanāya khau, vāhana…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya khau, vāhana… saṅkhāraihi khau, vāhana… viñ‍ñāṇaina khau, vāhana… jātiyā khau, vāhana… jarāya khau, vāhana… maraṇaina khau, vāhana… dukkhaihi khau, vāhana… kilaisaihi khau, vāhana, tathāgatau nissaṭau visaṃyuttau vippamuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| saiyyathāpi, vāhana, uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā udakai jātaṃ udakai saṃvaḍḍhaṃ udakā pac‍cuggamma ṭhitaṃ anupalittaṃ udakaina; aivamaivaṃ khau, vāhana, imaihi dasahi dhammaihi tathāgatau nissaṭau visaṃyuttau vippamuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ānandasuttaṃ

82. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sau  vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘assaddhau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘dussīlau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘appassutau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘dubbacau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ  virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti  naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘pāpamittau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘kusītau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘muṭṭhassati samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘asantuṭṭhau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘pāpicchau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘micchādiṭṭhikau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘imaihi dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘saddhau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘sīlavā samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘bahussutau sutadharau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘suvacau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘kalyāṇamittau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai  vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘āraddhavīriyau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘upaṭṭhitassati samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti  ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘santuṭṭhau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘appicchau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘sammādiṭṭhikau samānau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu ‘imaihi dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. puṇṇiyasuttaṃ

83. atha khau āyasmā puṇṇiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā puṇṇiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina appaikadā tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti appaikadā nappaṭibhātī’’ti?

‘‘saddhau ca, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca upasaṅkamitā; naiva tāva tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti| yatau ca khau, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti upasaṅkamitā ca, aivaṃ tathāgataṃ  dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti|

‘‘saddhau ca, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu hauti upasaṅkamitā ca, nau ca payirupāsitā…pai॰… payirupāsitā ca, nau ca paripucchitā… paripucchitā ca, nau ca auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti… auhitasautau ca dhammaṃ suṇāti, nau ca sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti… sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhāraiti, nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati… dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya  dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti… atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau  ca hauti, nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā… kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, nau ca sandassakau hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ, naiva tāva tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, upasaṅkamitā ca, payirupāsitā ca, paripucchitā ca, auhitasautau ca dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhāraiti, dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau  ca hauti, kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ – aivaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti| imaihi khau, puṇṇiya, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā saman‍nāgatau (ka॰) aikantaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhātīti (syā॰) aikantapaṭibhānā aikantapaṭibhānaṃ (sī॰) tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā hautī’’ti aikantaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhātīti (syā॰)| tatiyaṃ|

4. byākaraṇasuttaṃ

84. tatra khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau  vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau samanuyuñ‍jati samanuggāhati samanubhāsati| sau tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalaina paracittakusalaina paracittapariyāyakusalaina samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanuggāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau irīṇaṃ āpaj‍jati vicinaṃ visinaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) āpaj‍jati anayaṃ āpaj‍jati byasanaṃ āpaj‍jati anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘tamainaṃ  tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau ayamāyasmā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti?

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘kaudhanau  khau ayamāyasmā; kaudhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| kaudhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai  parihānamaitaṃ|

‘upanāhī khau pana ayamāyasmā; upanāhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| upanāhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘makkhī khau pana ayamāyasmā; makkhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| makkhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘paḷāsī khau pana ayamāyasmā; paḷāsapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| paḷāsapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘issukī khau pana ayamāyasmā; issāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| issāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘maccharī khau pana ayamāyasmā; macchairapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| macchairapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau  pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘saṭhau khau pana ayamāyasmā; sāṭhaiyyapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| sāṭhaiyyapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘māyāvī  khau pana ayamāyasmā; māyāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| māyāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘pāpicchau khau pana ayamāyasmā; icchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| icchāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘sati  muṭṭhassati (sī॰ syā॰) khau pana ayamāyasmā uttari karaṇīyai auramattakaina visaisādhigamaina antarā vausānaṃ āpan‍nau| antarā vausānagamanaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ’|

‘‘sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu ‘imai dasa dhammai appahāya  imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu ‘imai dasa dhammai pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. katthīsuttaṃ

85. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahācundau caitīsu viharati sahajātiyaṃ| tatra khau āyasmā mahācundau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahācundassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahācundau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu katthī hauti vikatthī adhigamaisu – ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau  samanuyuñ‍jati samanuggāhati samanubhāsati| sau tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalaina paracittakusalaina paracittapariyāyakusalaina samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanuggāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau irīṇaṃ āpaj‍jati vicinaṃ āpaj‍jati anayaṃ āpaj‍jati byasanaṃ āpaj‍jati anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati|
