‘‘tamainaṃ  tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau ayamāyasmā katthī hauti vikatthī adhigamaisu – ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi…pai॰… ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘dīgharattaṃ khau ayamāyasmā khaṇḍakārī chiddakārī sabalakārī kammāsakārī na santatakārī na santatavutti sīlaisu| dussīlau khau ayamāyasmā| dussilyaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘assaddhau khau pana ayamāyasmā; assaddhiyaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘appassutau khau pana ayamāyasmā anācārau; appasac‍caṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘dubbacau khau pana ayamāyasmā; dauvacassatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘pāpamittau khau pana ayamāyasmā; pāpamittatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘kusītau  khau pana ayamāyasmā; kausaj‍jaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘muṭṭhassati khau pana ayamāyasmā; muṭṭhassac‍caṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘kuhakau khau pana ayamāyasmā; kauhañ‍ñaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘dubbharau khau pana ayamāyasmā; dubbharatā khau  pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘duppañ‍ñau  khau pana ayamāyasmā; duppañ‍ñatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sahāyakau sahāyakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yadā tai, samma, dhanaina bandhau (ka॰) dhanakaraṇīyaṃ assa, yācaiyyāsi maṃ yācissasi maṃ (sī॰), pavaidaiyyāsi maṃ (syā॰), parājaiyyāpi maṃ (ka॰) dhanaṃ| dassāmi tai dhana’nti| sau kiñ‍cidaiva dhanakaraṇīyai samuppan‍nai sahāyakau sahāyakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthau mai, samma, dhanaina| daihi mai dhana’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘taina hi, samma, idha khanāhī’ti| sau tatra khanantau nādhigacchaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘alikaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca; tucchakaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca – idha khanāhī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘nāhaṃ taṃ, samma, alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ avacaṃ| taina hi, samma, idha khanāhī’ti| sau tatrapi khanantau nādhigacchaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘alikaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca, tucchakaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca – idha khanāhī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘nāhaṃ taṃ, samma, alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ avacaṃ| taina hi, samma, idha khanāhī’ti| sau tatrapi khanantau nādhigacchaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘alikaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca, tucchakaṃ maṃ, samma, avaca – idha khanāhī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘nāhaṃ taṃ, samma, alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ avacaṃ| api ca ahamaiva ummādaṃ pāpuṇiṃ caitasau vipariyāya’nti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, āvusau, bhikkhu katthī hauti vikatthī adhigamaisu – ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi  vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipi, ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau samanuyuñ‍jati samanuggāhati samanubhāsati| sau tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalaina paracittakusalaina paracittapariyāyakusalaina samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanuggāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau irīṇaṃ  āpaj‍jati vicinaṃ āpaj‍jati anayaṃ āpaj‍jati byasanaṃ āpaj‍jati anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau ayamāyasmā katthī hauti vikatthī adhigamaisu – ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi…pai॰… ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmipi vuṭṭhahāmipī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘dīgharattaṃ  khau ayamāyasmā khaṇḍakārī chiddakārī sabalakārī kammāsakārī, na santatakārī na santatavutti sīlaisu| dussīlau khau ayamāyasmā; dussilyaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘assaddhau khau pana ayamāyasmā; assaddhiyaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘appassutau  khau pana ayamāyasmā anācārau; appasac‍caṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘dubbacau khau pana ayamāyasmā; dauvacassatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘pāpamittau khau pana ayamāyasmā; pāpamittatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘kusītau khau pana ayamāyasmā; kausaj‍jaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘muṭṭhassati khau pana ayamāyasmā; muṭṭhassac‍caṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘kuhakau khau pana ayamāyasmā; kauhañ‍ñaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘dubbharau  khau pana ayamāyasmā; dubbharatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘duppañ‍ñau khau pana ayamāyasmā; duppañ‍ñatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ’|

‘‘sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu ‘imai dasa dhammai appahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu ‘imai dasa dhammai pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. adhimānasuttaṃ

86. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau āyasmā mahākassapau  bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākassapassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahākassapau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ  byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau samanuyuñ‍jati samanuggāhati samanubhāsati| sau tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalaina paracittakusalaina paracittapariyāyakusalaina samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanuggāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau irīṇaṃ āpaj‍jati vicinaṃ āpaj‍jati anayaṃ āpaj‍jati byasanaṃ āpaj‍jati anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau ayamāyasmā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ  tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘adhimānikau khau ayamāyasmā adhimānasac‍cau, appattai pattasañ‍ñī, akatai katasañ‍ñī, anadhigatai adhigatasañ‍ñī| adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau  aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau ayamāyasmā nissāya adhimānikau adhimānasac‍cau, appattai pattasañ‍ñī, akatai katasañ‍ñī, anadhigatai adhigatasañ‍ñī| adhimānaina  añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘bahussutau khau pana ayamāyasmā sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| tasmā ayamāyasmā adhimānikau adhimānasac‍cau, appattai pattasañ‍ñī, akatai katasañ‍ñī, anadhigatai adhigatasañ‍ñī| adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā jhāyī samāpattikusalau paracittakusalau paracittapariyāyakusalau aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti –

‘abhijjhālu khau pana ayamāyasmā; abhijjhāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| abhijjhāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘byāpan‍nau  khau pana ayamāyasmā; byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| byāpādapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘thinamiddhau khau pana ayamāyasmā; thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘uddhatau  khau pana ayamāyasmā; uddhac‍capariyuṭṭhitaina  caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| uddhac‍capariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘vicikicchau khau pana ayamāyasmā; vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharati| vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘kammārāmau khau pana ayamāyasmā kammaratau kammārāmataṃ anuyuttau| kammārāmatā  khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘bhassārāmau khau pana ayamāyasmā bhassaratau bhassārāmataṃ anuyuttau| bhassārāmatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘niddārāmau khau pana ayamāyasmā niddāratau niddārāmataṃ anuyuttau| niddārāmatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘saṅgaṇikārāmau khau pana ayamāyasmā saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau| saṅgaṇikārāmatā khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ|

‘sati khau pana ayamāyasmā uttari karaṇīyai auramattakaina visaisādhigamaina antarā vausānaṃ āpan‍nau| antarā vausānagamanaṃ khau pana tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai parihānamaitaṃ’|

‘‘sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu ‘imai dasa dhammai appahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu  ‘imai dasa dhammai pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nappiyasuttaṃ

87. tatra  khau bhagavā kālaṅkataṃ bhikkhuṃ kalandakaṃ bhikkhuṃ (sī॰), kāḷakabhikkhuṃ (syā॰) ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu adhikaraṇikau hauti, adhikaraṇasamathassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu adhikaraṇikau hauti adhikaraṇasamathassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na sikkhākāmau hauti, sikkhāsamādānassa sikkhākāmassa (ka॰) na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na sikkhākāmau hauti sikkhāsamādānassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāpicchau hauti, icchāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāpicchau hauti icchāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti, kaudhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu makkhī hauti, makkhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu makkhī hauti makkhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṭhau hauti, sāṭhaiyyavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṭhau hauti sāṭhaiyyavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu māyāvī hauti, māyāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu māyāvī hauti māyāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammānaṃ na nisāmakajātikau hauti, dhammanisantiyā na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammānaṃ na nisāmakajātikau hauti dhammanisantiyā na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paṭisal‍līnau hauti, paṭisal‍lānassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paṭisal‍līnau hauti paṭisal‍lānassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ na paṭisanthārakau paṭisandhārakau (ka॰) hauti, paṭisanthārakassa na vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ na paṭisanthārakau hauti paṭisanthārakassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau na piyatāya na garutāya na bhāvanāya na sāmañ‍ñāya na aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata  maṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ garukaraiyyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰) mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī na caiva sak‍karaunti na garuṃ karaunti garukaraunti (sī॰ syā॰) na mānainti na pūjainti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai appahīnai samanupassanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkassa kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata maṃ  manussā ājānīyaṭṭhānai ṭhapaiyyuṃ, ājānīyabhaujanañ‍ca bhaujaiyyuṃ, ājānīyaparimaj‍janañ‍ca parimaj‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ manussā na  caiva ājānīyaṭṭhānai ṭhapainti na ca ājānīyabhaujanaṃ bhaujainti na ca ājānīyaparimaj‍janaṃ parimaj‍janti| taṃ  kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū manussā tāni sāṭhaiyyāni kūṭaiyyāni jimhaiyyāni vaṅkaiyyāni appahīnāni samanupassanti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aivarūpassa bhikkhunau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī na caiva sak‍karaunti na garuṃ karaunti na mānainti na pūjainti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai appahīnai samanupassanti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na adhikaraṇikau hauti, adhikaraṇasamathassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na adhikaraṇikau hauti adhikaraṇasamathassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau piyatāya garutāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhākāmau hauti, sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhākāmau hauti  sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau piyatāya garutāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appicchau hauti, icchāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appicchau hauti icchāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kaudhanau hauti, kaudhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kaudhanau hauti kaudhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu amakkhī hauti, makkhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu amakkhī hauti makkhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaṭhau hauti, sāṭhaiyyavinayassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaṭhau hauti sāṭhaiyyavinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu amāyāvī hauti, māyāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu amāyāvī hauti māyāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammānaṃ nisāmakajātikau hauti, dhammanisantiyā vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammānaṃ nisāmakajātikau hauti dhammanisantiyā vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisal‍līnau hauti, paṭisal‍lānassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisal‍līnau hauti paṭisal‍lānassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya  saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ paṭisanthārakau hauti, paṭisanthārakassa vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ paṭisanthārakau hauti paṭisanthārakassa vaṇṇavādī, ayampi dhammau piyatāya garutāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai pahīnai samanupassanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bhaddassa assājānīyassa kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata maṃ manussā ājānīyaṭṭhānai ṭhapaiyyuṃ, ājānīyabhaujanañ‍ca bhaujaiyyuṃ, ājānīyaparimaj‍janañ‍ca parimaj‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ manussā ājānīyaṭṭhānai ca ṭhapainti ājānīyabhaujanañ‍ca bhaujainti  ājānīyaparimaj‍janañ‍ca parimaj‍janti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū manussā tāni sāṭhaiyyāni kūṭaiyyāni jimhaiyyāni vaṅkaiyyāni pahīnāni samanupassanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aivarūpassa bhikkhunau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyu’nti, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhissa, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai pahīnai samanupassantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ak‍kausakasuttaṃ

88. ‘‘yau  sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ariyūpavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ ṭhānamaitaṃ avakāsau aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) yaṃ sau dasan‍naṃ byasanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ byasanaṃ nigacchaiyya na nigacchaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| katamaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ? anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati, adhigatā parihāyati, saddhammassa na vaudāyanti, saddhammaisu vā adhimānikau hauti anabhiratau vā brahmacariyaṃ carati, añ‍ñataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, gāḷhaṃ vā raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, ummādaṃ vā pāpuṇāti cittakkhaipaṃ, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ariyūpavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, ṭhānamaitaṃ avakāsau yaṃ sau imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ byasanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ byasanaṃ nigacchaiyyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kaukālikasuttaṃ

89. saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.181; su॰ ni॰ kaukālikasutta atha  khau kaukālikau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pāpicchā, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| ‘‘mā  haivaṃ, kaukālika, mā haivaṃ, kaukālika! pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi mai, bhantai, bhagavā saddhāyikau pac‍cayikau, atha khau pāpicchāva sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, mā haivaṃ, kaukālika! pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu  cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

tatiyampi khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi mai, bhantai, bhagavā saddhāyikau pac‍cayikau, atha khau pāpicchāva sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, mā haivaṃ, kaukālika! pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| acirapak‍kantassa ca kaukālikassa bhikkhunau sāsapamattīhi pīḷakāhi sabbau kāyau phuṭau ahausi| sāsapamattiyau hutvā muggamattiyau ahaisuṃ, muggamattiyau hutvā kalāyamattiyau ahaisuṃ, kalāyamattiyau hutvā kaulaṭṭhimattiyau ahaisuṃ, kaulaṭṭhimattiyau hutvā kaulamattiyau ahaisuṃ, kaulamattiyau hutvā āmalakamattiyau ahaisuṃ, āmalakamattiyau hutvā (tiṇḍukamattiyau ahaisuṃ, tiṇḍukamattiyau hutvā,) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.181; su॰ ni॰ kaukālikasutta natthi baiḷuvasalāṭukamattiyau ahaisuṃ, baiḷuvasalāṭukamattiyau hutvā bil‍lamattiyau ahaisuṃ, bil‍lamattiyau hutvā pabhij‍jiṃsu, pubbañ‍ca  lauhitañ‍ca pagghariṃsu| sau sudaṃ kadalipattaisu saiti macchauva visagilitau|

atha khau turū pac‍caikabrahmā tuduppac‍caikabrahmā (sī॰ pī॰), tudi pac‍caikabrahmā (syā॰), turi pac‍caikabrahmā (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.180 yaina kaukālikau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vaihāsai ṭhatvā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti| ‘‘kausi tvaṃ, āvusau’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ turū pac‍caikabrahmā’’ti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā anāgāmī byākatau, atha kiñ‍carahi idhāgatau? passa yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddha’’nti|

atha  khau turū  pac‍caikabrahmā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘purisassa hi jātassa, kuṭhārī jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati, taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ, kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ kali, yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā, ayamaiva mahattarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padūsayai||

‘‘sataṃ  sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ, chattiṃsati pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī nirayaṃ upaiti, vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti||

atha  khau kaukālikau bhikkhu tainaiva ābādhaina kālamakāsi| kālaṅkatau ca kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā|

atha khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaukālikau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| kālaṅkatau ca, bhantai, kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau  khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kaukālikau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; kālaṅkatau ca, bhantai, kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīva dīghaṃ nu khau, bhantai, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇa’’nti? ‘‘dīghaṃ khau, bhikkhu, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇaṃ| na taṃ sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakāni vassānīti  vā aittakāni vassasatānīti vā aittakāni vassasahassānīti vā aittakāni vassasatasahassānīti vā’’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhū,’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau tatau purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina aikamaikaṃ tilaṃ uddharaiyya| khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhu, vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau iminā  upak‍kamaina parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, na tvaiva aikau abbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati abbudā nirayā, aivamaikau nirabbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati nirabbudā nirayā, aivamaikau ababau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ababā nirayā, aivamaikau aṭaṭau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā, aivamaikau ahahau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ahahā nirayā, aivamaikau kumudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati kumudā nirayā, aivamaikau saugandhikau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati saugandhikā nirayā, aivamaikau uppalakau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati uppalakā nirayā, aivamaikau puṇḍarīkau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati puṇḍarīkā nirayā, aivamaikau padumau nirayau| padumaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, nirayaṃ kaukālikau bhikkhu upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘purisassa  hi jātassa, kuṭhārī jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘yau  nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati, taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ, kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ kali, yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā, ayamaiva mahattarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padūsayai||

‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ, chattiṃsati pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī nirayaṃ upaiti, vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. khīṇāsavabalasuttaṃ

90. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, sāriputta, khīṇāsavassa  bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti?

‘‘dasa, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti| katamāni dasa? a॰ ni॰ 8.28; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.44 idha, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā  haunti, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ naikkhammābhirataṃ byantībhūtaṃ sabbasau āsavaṭṭhāniyaihi dhammaihi| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ naikkhammābhirataṃ byantībhūtaṃ sabbasau āsavaṭṭhāniyaihi dhammaihi, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā…pai॰… cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā  …pai॰… pañ‍cindriyāni… pañ‍ca balāni bhāvitāni  haunti subhāvitāni… satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu  āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhantai, dasa khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

thairavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

vāhanānandau puṇṇiyau, byākaraṃ katthimānikau|

napiyak‍kausakaukāli, khīṇāsavabalaina cāti||


(10) 5. upālivaggau

1. kāmabhaugīsuttaṃ

91. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘dasayimai, gahapati, kāmabhaugī santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai dasa? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina; adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha  pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina; adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina; adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi; dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi; dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi; dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina  attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| tai ca bhaugai gathitau gadhitau (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.124 passitabbaṃ mucchitau  ajjhausan‍nau ajjhāpan‍nau (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 3.124 suttavaṇṇanā ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau  paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| tai ca bhaugai agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina  gārayhau| ‘na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti  pīṇaitī’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau, imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau aikaina  ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainā’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaitī’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘na saṃvibhajati  na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, tai ca bhaugai gathitau mucchitau ajjhausan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina  ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘tai ca bhaugai gathitau mucchitau ajjhausan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jatī’ti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gahapati, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, tai ca bhaugai agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati, ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī catūhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau| ‘dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasainā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitī’ti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautī’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ‘tai ca bhaugai agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jatī’ti, iminā catutthaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gahapati, kāmabhaugī imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau|

‘‘imai khau, gahapati, dasa kāmabhaugī santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, gahapati, dasan‍naṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina  bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, tai ca bhaugai  agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati, ayaṃ imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau maukkhau (ka॰ sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.95; 5.181; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.662 ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau| sappimaṇḍau tattha aggamakkhāyati|

aivamaivaṃ khau, gahapati, imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati  puñ‍ñāni karauti, tai ca bhaugai agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati, ayaṃ imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau maukkhau (ka॰ sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.181 ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bhayasuttaṃ

92. a॰ ni॰ 9.27; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1024 atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yatau, khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ariyau cassa ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau| sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, gahapati, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikampi naiva diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati na samparāyikampi na samparāyikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati na caitasikampi na caitasikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārī… musāvādī… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati na samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ  taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti; dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti; saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti; ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti  ‘akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi  samādhisaṃvattanikaihi’| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘katamau cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti; imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti; imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti; avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa  niraudhau hautī’ti| ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau|

‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi ca catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau  pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kiṃdiṭṭhikasuttaṃ

93. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati divā divassa sāvatthiyā nikkhami bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau tāva bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā| manaubhāvanīyānampi bhikkhūnaṃ akālau dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnā manaubhāvanīyā bhikkhū| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā saṅgamma samāgamma un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā nisin‍nā haunti| addasaṃsu khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṇṭhāpaisuṃ – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati ārāmaṃ āgacchati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau| yāvatā khau pana samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti, ayaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati| appasaddakāmā  khau pana tai āyasmantau appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādinau| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

atha  khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ  vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘vadaihi, gahapati, kiṃdiṭṭhikau samaṇau gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāmī’’ti|

‘‘iti kira tvaṃ, gahapati, na samaṇassa gautamassa sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi; vadaihi, gahapati, kiṃdiṭṭhikā bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘bhikkhūnampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, na sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāmī’’ti|

‘‘iti kira tvaṃ, gahapati, na samaṇassa gautamassa sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi napi bhikkhūnaṃ sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi; vadaihi, gahapati, kiṃdiṭṭhikausi tuva’’nti? ‘‘aitaṃ khau, bhantai, amhaihi na duk‍karaṃ byākātuṃ yaṃdiṭṭhikā mayaṃ| iṅgha tāva āyasmantau yathāsakāni diṭṭhigatāni byākarauntu, pacchāpaitaṃ amhaihi na duk‍karaṃ bhavissati byākātuṃ yaṃdiṭṭhikā maya’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau paribbājakau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’’ti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau  paribbājakau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’’ti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau paribbājakau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati tai paribbājakai aitadavauca – ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā aivamāha – ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’ti, imassa ayamāyasmatau diṭṭhi attanau vā ayaunisaumanasikārahaitu uppan‍nā parataughausapac‍cayā vā| sā khau panaisā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṅkhatā caitayitā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadaivaisau āyasmā al‍līnau, tadaivaisau āyasmā ajjhupagatau|

‘‘yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā aivamāha – ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’ti, imassāpi ayamāyasmatau diṭṭhi attanau vā ayaunisaumanasikārahaitu uppan‍nā parataughausapac‍cayā vā| sā khau panaisā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṅkhatā caitayitā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadaivaisau āyasmā al‍līnau, tadaivaisau āyasmā ajjhupagatau|

‘‘yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā aivamāha – ‘antavā laukau  …pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, gahapatī’ti, imassāpi ayamāyasmatau diṭṭhi attanau vā ayaunisaumanasikārahaitu uppan‍nā parataughausapac‍cayā vā| sā khau panaisā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṅkhatā caitayitā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadaivaisau āyasmā al‍līnau, tadaivaisau āyasmā ajjhupagatau’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai tai paribbājakā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘byākatāni khau, gahapati, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakāni diṭṭhigatāni| vadaihi, gahapati, kiṃdiṭṭhikausi tuva’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| ‘yaṃ dukkhaṃ taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivaṃdiṭṭhikau ahaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ  khau, gahapati, kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadaiva tvaṃ, gahapati, al‍līnau, tadaiva tvaṃ, gahapati, ajjhupagatau’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ caitayitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ tadanic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| ‘yaṃ dukkhaṃ taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, namaisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ| tassa ca uttari nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai tai paribbājakā tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā nisīdiṃsu| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati tai paribbājakai tuṇhībhūtai maṅkubhūtai pattakkhandhai adhaumukhai pajjhāyantai appaṭibhānai viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yāvatakau ahausi taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, gahapati! aivaṃ khau tai, gahapati, maughapurisā kālaina kālaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitabbā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantai anāthapiṇḍikai gahapatimhi bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaupi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vassasatupasampan‍nau bhikkhu dīgharattaṃ avaidhi dhammau (syā॰) imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, saupi aivamaivaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhaiyya yathā taṃ anāthapiṇḍikaina gahapatinā niggahitā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vaj‍jiyamāhitasuttaṃ

94. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| atha khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati divā divassa campāya nikkhami bhagavantaṃ  dassanāya| atha khau vaj‍jiyamāhitassa gahapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau tāva bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā| manaubhāvanīyānampi bhikkhūnaṃ akālau dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnā manaubhāvanīyāpi bhikkhū| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā saṅgamma samāgamma un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā nisin‍nā haunti|

addasaṃsu khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā vaj‍jiyamāhitaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṇṭhāpaisuṃ – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu| mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati āgacchati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau| yāvatā khau pana  samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā campāyaṃ paṭivasanti, ayaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati| appasaddakāmā khau pana tai āyasmantau appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādinau| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati yaina tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau vaj‍jiyamāhitaṃ gahapatiṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ  kira, gahapati, samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausati upavadatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, bhantai, bhagavā sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati napi sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausati upavadati| gārayhaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā garahati, pasaṃsitabbaṃ pasaṃsati| gārayhaṃ khau pana, bhantai, bhagavā garahantau pasaṃsitabbaṃ pasaṃsantau vibhaj‍javādau bhagavā| na sau bhagavā aittha aikaṃsavādau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau paribbājakau vaj‍jiyamāhitaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, gahapati, yassa tvaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, samaṇau gautamau vainayikau appañ‍ñattikau’’ti? ‘‘aitthapāhaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantai vakkhāmi sahadhammaina – ‘idaṃ kusala’nti, bhantai, bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ; ‘idaṃ akusala’nti, bhantai, bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ| iti kusalākusalaṃ bhagavā pañ‍ñāpayamānau sapañ‍ñattikau bhagavā; na sau bhagavā vainayikau appañ‍ñattikau’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai tai paribbājakā tuṇhībhūtā  maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā nisīdiṃsu| atha khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati tai paribbājakai tuṇhībhūtai maṅkubhūtai pattakkhandhai adhaumukhai pajjhāyantai appaṭibhānai viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati yāvatakau ahausi taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, gahapati! aivaṃ  khau tai, gahapati, maughapurisā kālaina kālaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitabbā| nāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ tapaṃ tapitabbanti vadāmi; na ca panāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ tapaṃ na tapitabbanti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ samādānaṃ samāditabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ samādānaṃ na samāditabbanti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ padhānaṃ padahitabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbaṃ padhānaṃ na padahitabbanti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbau paṭinissaggau paṭinissaj‍jitabbauti vadāmi| na panāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbau paṭinissaggau na paṭinissaj‍jitabbauti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbā vimutti vimuc‍citabbāti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, gahapati, sabbā vimutti na vimuc‍citabbāti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, gahapati, tapaṃ tapatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ tapaṃ na tapitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa gahapati, tapaṃ tapatau akusalā  dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā  abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ tapaṃ tapitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, gahapati, samādānaṃ samādiyatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ samādānaṃ na samāditabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, gahapati, samādānaṃ samādiyatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ samādānaṃ samāditabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, gahapati, padhānaṃ padahatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ padhānaṃ na padahitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, gahapati, padhānaṃ padahatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ padhānaṃ padahitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, gahapati, paṭinissaggaṃ paṭinissaj‍jatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpau paṭinissaggau na paṭinissaj‍jitabbauti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, gahapati, paṭinissaggaṃ paṭinissaj‍jatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpau paṭinissaggau paṭinissaj‍jitabbauti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, gahapati, vimuttiṃ vimuc‍catau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpā vimutti na vimuc‍citabbāti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, gahapati, vimuttiṃ vimuc‍catau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpā vimutti vimuc‍citabbāti vadāmī’’ti|

atha khau vaj‍jiyamāhitau gahapati bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantai vaj‍jiyamāhitai gahapatimhi bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaupi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, saupi aivamaivaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyai  paribbājakai sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhaiyya yathā taṃ vaj‍jiyamāhitaina gahapatinā niggahitā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. uttiyasuttaṃ

95. atha  khau uttiyau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uttiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sassatau laukau, idamaiva  sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, uttiya, mayā – ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, uttiya, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā … na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, uttiya, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, uttiya, mayā – sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘aitampi khau, uttiya, abyākataṃ mayā asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau gautama, antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva  sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti, iti puṭṭhau samānau  – ‘aitampi khau, uttiya, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| atha kiñ‍carahi bhautā gautamaina byākata’’nti?

‘‘abhiñ‍ñāya khau ahaṃ, uttiya, sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ panaitaṃ bhavaṃ gautamau abhiñ‍ñāya sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, sabbau vā sabbau ca (ka॰) taina laukau nīyati nīyissati (sī॰), niyyāssati (syā॰), niyyaṃssati (pī॰) upaḍḍhau vā tibhāgau vā’’ti tibhāgau vāti padaihi (ka॰)? aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi|

atha  khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mā haivaṃ khau uttiyau paribbājakau pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ paṭilabhi – ‘sabbasāmuk‍kaṃsikaṃ vata mai samaṇau gautamau pañhaṃ puṭṭhau saṃsādaiti, nau vissaj‍jaiti, na nūna visahatī’ti| tadassa uttiyassa paribbājakassa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau uttiyaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tainahāvusau uttiya, upamaṃ tai karissāmi| upamāya midhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau uttiya, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ daḷhuddhāpaṃ daḷhuddāpaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) daḷhapākāratauraṇaṃ aikadvāraṃ| tatrassa dauvārikau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā ñātānaṃ  pavaisaitā| sau tassa nagarassa samantā anupariyāyapathaṃ anuk‍kamati| anupariyāyapathaṃ anuk‍kamamānau na passaiyya pākārasandhiṃ vā pākāravivaraṃ  vā, antamasau biḷāranikkhamanamattampi| nau ca khvassa aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘aittakā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā’ti| atha  khvassa aivamaittha hauti – ‘yai khau kaici auḷārikā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā, sabbai tai iminā dvāraina pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau uttiya, na tathāgatassa aivaṃ ussuk‍kaṃ hauti – ‘sabbau vā taina laukau nīyati, upaḍḍhau vā, tibhāgau vā’ti| atha khau aivamaittha tathāgatassa hauti – ‘yai khau kaici laukamhā nīyiṃsu vā nīyanti vā nīyissanti vā, sabbai tai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā, satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā| aivamaitai aivamaitaina (ka॰) laukamhā nīyiṃsu vā nīyanti vā nīyissanti vā’ti| yadaiva khau tvaṃ yadaiva khvaittha (ka॰), āvusau uttiya, bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ imaṃ pañhaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) apucchi tadaivaitaṃ pañhaṃ bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñaina pariyāyaina apucchi| tasmā tai taṃ bhagavā na byākāsī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kaukanudasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau rājagahai viharati tapaudārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudā tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| tapaudāya tapaudai (ka॰) gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau| kaukanudaupi khau paribbājakau rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudā tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ|

addasā khau kaukanudau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘k‍vaittha kau taittha (sī॰), k‍vattha (pī॰ ka॰), āvusau’’ti? ‘‘ahamāvusau, bhikkhū’’ti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhūna’’nti? ‘‘samaṇānaṃ, āvusau, sakyaputtiyāna’’nti|

‘‘pucchaiyyāma mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva daisaṃ, sacai āyasmā aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘pucchāvusau, sutvā vaidissāmā’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau, ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhikau (syā॰) bhava’’nti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau, ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhava’’nti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau, antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhava’’nti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘taina  hi bhavaṃ na jānāti, na passatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, na jānāmi na passāmi| jānāmahaṃ, āvusau, passāmī’’ti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau, sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhava’nti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau, asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhava’nti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau, antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavanti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘‘taina hi bhavaṃ na jānāti na passatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, na jānāmi na passāmi| jānāmahaṃ, āvusau, passāmī’ti vadaisi| yathā kathaṃ panāvusau, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti khau, āvusau, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ| ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti khau, āvusau, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ| antavā laukau…pai॰… anantavā laukau… taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… na hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti  khau, āvusau, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ|

‘‘yāvatā, āvusau, diṭṭhi diṭṭhigatā (sabbattha) yāvatā diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ diṭṭhiadhiṭṭhānaṃ diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānaṃ diṭṭhisamuṭṭhānaṃ diṭṭhisamugghātau yāvatā diṭṭhiṭṭhāna adhiṭṭhāna pariyuṭṭhāna samuṭṭhāna samugghātau (sī॰ pī॰), tamahaṃ jānāmi tamahaṃ passāmi| tamahaṃ jānantau tamahaṃ passantau kyāhaṃ vakkhāmi – ‘na jānāmi na passāmī’ti? jānāmahaṃ, āvusau, passāmī’’ti|

‘‘kau nāmau āyasmā, kathañ‍ca panāyasmantaṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti? ‘‘‘ānandau’ti khau mai, āvusau, nāmaṃ| ‘ānandau’ti ca pana maṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti| ‘‘mahācariyaina vata kira, bhau, saddhiṃ mantayamānā na jānimha – ‘āyasmā ānandau’ti| sacai hi mayaṃ jānaiyyāma – ‘ayaṃ āyasmā ānandau’ti, aittakampi nau nappaṭibhāyaiyya nappaṭibhāsaiyyāma (ka॰) nappaṭibhāsaiyya (bahūsu) ma॰ ni॰ 3.216 passitabbaṃ| khamatu ca mai āyasmā ānandau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. āhunaiyyasuttaṃ

97. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘katamaihi  dasahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ  kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā|

‘‘kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikau hauti sammādassanaina saman‍nāgatau|

‘‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti; bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ  tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimasati (sī॰) parimaj‍jati, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti| sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

‘‘anaikavihitaṃ  pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi  jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi  jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata khau bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina  saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. thairasuttaṃ

98. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati, phāsuyaiva viharati| katamaihi dasahi? thairau hauti rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau, sīlavā hauti …pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhau, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, adhikaraṇasamuppādavūpasamakusalau hauti, dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, pāsādikau hauti abhik‍kantapaṭik‍kantai abhik‍kantapaṭik‍kantau (ka॰) susaṃvutau antaragharai nisaj‍jāya, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  thairau bhikkhu yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati, phāsuyaiva viharatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upālisuttaṃ

99. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivitu’’nti|

‘‘durabhisambhavāni hi khau durabhisambhavāni khau (sī॰ pī॰), upāli, arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni| duk‍karaṃ pavivaikaṃ durabhiramaṃ| aikattai haranti mañ‍ñai manau vanāni samādhiṃ alabhamānassa bhikkhunau| yau khau, upāli, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ samādhiṃ alabhamānau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivissāmī’ti, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘saṃsīdissati vā up‍lavissati vā’ti uppilavissati vā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, upāli, mahāudakarahadau| atha āgacchaiyya hatthināgau sattaratanau vā aḍḍhaṭṭharatanau aṭṭharatanau (sī॰ pī॰) vā| tassa aivamassa – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ udakarahadaṃ augāhaitvā kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyyaṃ piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyyaṃ| kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā nhatvā nahātvā (sī॰ pī॰), nhātvā (syā॰) ca pivitvā ca pac‍cuttaritvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamaiyya’nti| sau taṃ udakarahadaṃ augāhaitvā kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyya piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyya; kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā  nhatvā ca pivitvā ca pac‍cuttaritvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? mahā, upāli mahā hupāli (sī॰ pī॰), attabhāvau gambhīrai gādhaṃ vindati|

‘‘atha āgacchaiyya sasau vā biḷārau vā| tassa aivamassa – ‘kau  cāhaṃ, kau ca hatthināgau! yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ udakarahadaṃ augāhaitvā kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyyaṃ piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷaiyyaṃ; kaṇṇasaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā piṭṭhisaṃdhauvikampi khiḍḍaṃ kīḷitvā nhatvā ca pivitvā ca pac‍cuttaritvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamaiyya’nti| sau taṃ udakarahadaṃ sahasā appaṭisaṅkhā pakkhandaiyya| tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘saṃsīdissati vā up‍lavissati vā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? parittau, upāli, attabhāvau gambhīrai gādhaṃ na vindati| aivamaivaṃ khau, upāli, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ samādhiṃ alabhamānau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivissāmī’ti, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘saṃsīdissati vā up‍lavissati vā’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, upāli, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau sakaina muttakarīsaina kīḷati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, nanvāyaṃ kaivalā paripūrā bālakhiḍḍā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sa khau sau, upāli, kumārau aparaina samayaina vuddhimanvāya indriyānaṃ paripākamanvāya yāni kānici kumārakānaṃ kīḷāpanakāni bhavanti, saiyyathidaṃ – vaṅkakaṃ vaṅkaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ghaṭikaṃ maukkhacikaṃ ciṅgulakaṃ piṅgulikaṃ (syā॰), ciṅkulakaṃ (ka॰) pattāḷhakaṃ rathakaṃ dhanukaṃ, taihi kīḷati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, nanvāyaṃ khiḍḍā purimāya khiḍḍāya abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā cā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sa khau sau, upāli, kumārau aparaina samayaina vuddhimanvāya indriyānaṃ paripākamanvāya pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgibhūtau paricāraiti cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi  rajanīyaihi, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi  iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, nanvāyaṃ khiḍḍā purimāhi khiḍḍāhi abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā cā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

dī॰ ni॰ 1.190; ma॰ ni॰ 2.233 ‘‘idha  khau pana vau vauti nipātamattaṃ (aṭṭha॰), upāli, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti|

‘‘taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau  taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti|

‘‘sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni  acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī; athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī  viratau maithunā gāmadhammā|

‘‘musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ  vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī; samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

‘‘sau  bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti| aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau, viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti, mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti, jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti, tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti, uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati, saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti| aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina  piṇḍapātaina| yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau  abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina  indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñai saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

‘‘sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau  sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, nau ca khau tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni  pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, nau ca khau tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, nau ca khau tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…|

puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi  khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, nau ca khau tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti|
