‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘santamaitaṃ paṇītamaita’nti  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi  khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, nau ca khau tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, upāli, ‘nanvāyaṃ vihārau purimaihi vihāraihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imampi khau, upāli, mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti, anuppattasadatthā ca viharanti| iṅgha tvaṃ, upāli, saṅghai viharāhi| saṅghai tai viharatau phāsu bhavissatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. abhabbasuttaṃ

100. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai dasa? rāgaṃ, dausaṃ, mauhaṃ, kaudhaṃ, upanāhaṃ, makkhaṃ, paḷāsaṃ, issaṃ, macchariyaṃ, mānaṃ – imai khau bhikkhavai, dasa dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai dasa? rāgaṃ, dausaṃ, mauhaṃ, kaudhaṃ, upanāhaṃ, makkhaṃ, paḷāsaṃ, issaṃ, macchariyaṃ, mānaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| dasamaṃ|

upālivaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kāmabhaugī  bhayaṃ diṭṭhi, vaj‍jiyamāhituttiyā|

kaukanudau āhunaiyyau, thairau upāli abhabbauti||

dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



3. tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(11) 1. samaṇasañ‍ñāvaggau

1. samaṇasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

101. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, samaṇasañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti| katamā tissau? vaivaṇṇiyamhi ajjhupagatau, parapaṭibaddhā mai jīvikā, añ‍ñau mai ākappau karaṇīyauti – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau samaṇasañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti|

‘‘katamai satta? santatakārī satatakārī (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) hauti santatavutti satatavutti (syā॰ pī॰) sīlaisu, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpaj‍jau hauti, anatimānī hauti, sikkhākāmau hauti, idamatthaṃtissa hauti jīvitaparikkhāraisu, āraddhavīriyau ca āraddhaviriyau ca (sī॰ pī॰), āraddhaviriyau (syā॰) viharati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau samaṇasañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā imai satta dhammai paripūraintī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. baujjhaṅgasuttaṃ

102. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tissau vij‍jā paripūrainti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tissau vij‍jā paripūrainti| katamā tissau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi  jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā imā tissau vij‍jā paripūraintī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. micchattasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘micchattaṃ, bhikkhavai, āgamma virādhanā hauti, nau ārādhanā| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, micchattaṃ āgamma virādhanā hauti, nau ārādhanā? micchādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau pahauti, micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācā pahauti, micchāvācassa micchākammantau  pahauti, micchākammantassa micchāājīvau pahauti, micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmau pahauti, micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati pahauti, micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahauti, micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇaṃ pahauti, micchāñāṇissa micchāñāṇassa (pī॰ ka॰) micchāvimutti pahauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, micchattaṃ āgamma virādhanā hauti, nau ārādhanā|

‘‘sammattaṃ, bhikkhavai, āgamma ārādhanā hauti, nau virādhanā| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammattaṃ āgamma ārādhanā hauti, nau virādhanā? sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau pahauti, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahauti, sammāvācassa sammākammantau pahauti, sammākammantassa sammāājīvau pahauti, sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmau pahauti, sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahauti, sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahauti, sammāsamādhissa  sammāñāṇaṃ pahauti, sammāñāṇissa sammāñāṇassa (pī॰ ka॰) sammāvimutti pahauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sammattaṃ āgamma ārādhanā hauti, nau virādhanā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bījasuttaṃ

104. a॰ ni॰ 1.306; kathā॰ 708 ‘‘micchādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalassa micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācassa micchākammantassa micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmassa micchāsatissa micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇissa micchāvimuttissa yañ‍ca kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ samādiṇṇaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca  paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa diṭṭhi hi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, pāpikā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nimbabījaṃ vā kausātakibījaṃ vā tittakālābubījaṃ vā al‍lāya pathaviyā nikkhittaṃ yañ‍caiva  pathavirasaṃ upādiyati yañ‍ca āpaurasaṃ upādiyati, sabbaṃ taṃ tittakattāya kaṭukattāya asātattāya saṃvattati| taṃ kissa haitu? bījañhi, bhikkhavai, pāpakaṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācassa micchākammantassa micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmassa micchāsatissa micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇissa micchāvimuttissa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa, bhikkhavai, pāpikā|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalassa sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācassa sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsatissa sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇissa sammāvimuttissa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa, bhikkhavai, bhaddikā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ucchubījaṃ vā sālibījaṃ vā muddikābījaṃ vā al‍lāya pathaviyā nikkhittaṃ yañ‍ca pathavirasaṃ upādiyati yañ‍ca āpaurasaṃ upādiyati sabbaṃ taṃ sātattāya madhurattāya asaicanakattāya saṃvattati| taṃ kissa haitu? bījañhi bhikkhavai, bhaddakaṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhikassa…pai॰  … sammāvimuttissa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa, bhikkhavai, bhaddikā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vij‍jāsuttaṃ

105. ‘‘avij‍jā, bhikkhavai, pubbaṅgamā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā, anvadaiva  ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ| avij‍jāgatassa, bhikkhavai, aviddasunau micchādiṭṭhi pahauti, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchāsaṅkappau pahauti, micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācā pahauti, micchāvācassa micchākammantau pahauti, micchākammantassa  micchāājīvau pahauti, micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmau pahauti, micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati pahauti, micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahauti, micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇaṃ pahauti, micchāñāṇissa micchāvimutti pahauti|

‘‘vij‍jā, bhikkhavai, pubbaṅgamā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā, anvadaiva hirauttappaṃ| vij‍jāgatassa, bhikkhavai, viddasunau sammādiṭṭhi pahauti, sammādiṭṭhikassa sammāsaṅkappau pahauti, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahauti, sammāvācassa sammākammantau pahauti, sammākammantassa sammāājīvau pahauti, sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmau pahauti, sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahauti, sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahauti, sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahauti, sammāñāṇissa sammāvimutti pahautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. nij‍jarasuttaṃ

106. dī॰ ni॰ 3.360 ‘‘dasayimāni, bhikkhavai, nij‍jaravatthūni| katamāni dasa? sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā hauti; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti; yai ca micchāsaṅkappapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā  sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāsaṅkappapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāvācassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā nij‍jiṇṇā hauti; yai ca micchāvācāpac‍cayā anaikai  pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāvācāpac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti; yai ca micchākammantapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammākammantapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāājīvassa, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti; yai ca micchāājīvapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāājīvapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti; yai ca micchāvāyāmapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāvāyāmapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati nij‍jiṇṇā hauti; yai ca micchāsatipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāsatipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi nij‍jiṇṇau hauti; yai ca micchāsamādhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti  tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāsamādhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā  dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāñāṇissa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti; yai ca micchāñāṇapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāñāṇapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāvimuttissa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti nij‍jiṇṇā hauti; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā  anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dasa nij‍jaravatthūnī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dhauvanasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇaisu janapadaisu dhauvanaṃ nāma| tattha hauti an‍nampi pānampi khaj‍jampi bhauj‍jampi laiyyampi paiyyampi nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhauvanaṃ; ‘naitaṃ natthī’ti vadāmi| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhauvanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya  na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘ahañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ dhauvanaṃ daisaissāmi, yaṃ dhauvanaṃ aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, yaṃ dhauvanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā  sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamañ‍ca taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ dhauvanaṃ, (yaṃ dhauvanaṃ) ( ) natthi syāmapautthakai aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya  sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, yaṃ dhauvanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti?

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi niddhautā hauti; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai  pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa niddhautā haunti; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau niddhautau hauti…pai॰… sammāvācassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā niddhautā hauti… sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau niddhautau hauti… sammāājīvassa, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau niddhautau hauti… sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau niddhautau hauti… sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati niddhautā hauti… sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi niddhautau hauti… sammāñāṇissa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ niddhautaṃ hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘sammāvimuttissa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti niddhautā hauti; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa niddhautā haunti; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā  ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| idaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ dhauvanaṃ aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, yaṃ dhauvanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍cantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. tikicchakasuttaṃ

108. ‘‘tikicchakā, bhikkhavai, viraicanaṃ dainti pittasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ paṭighātāya, saimhasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ paṭighātāya, vātasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ paṭighātāya| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, viraicanaṃ; ‘naitaṃ natthī’ti vadāmi| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, viraicanaṃ sampaj‍jatipi vipaj‍jatipi|

‘‘ahañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ viraicanaṃ daisaissāmi, yaṃ viraicanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ viraicanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā  sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamañ‍ca taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ viraicanaṃ, yaṃ viraicanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ viraicanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti?

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi virittā hauti; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa virittā haunti; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau virittau hauti…pai॰… sammāvācassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā virittā hauti… sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau virittau hauti… sammāājīvassa, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau virittau hauti… sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau virittau hauti… sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati virittā hauti… sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi virittau hauti… sammāñāṇissa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ virittaṃ hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘sammāvimuttissa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti virittā hauti; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa virittā haunti; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| idaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ viraicanaṃ yaṃ viraicanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ viraicanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti…pai॰… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍cantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vamanasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘tikicchakā, bhikkhavai, vamanaṃ dainti pittasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ  paṭighātāya, saimhasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ paṭighātāya, vātasamuṭṭhānānampi ābādhānaṃ paṭighātāya| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vamanaṃ; ‘naitaṃ natthī’ti vadāmi| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vamanaṃ sampaj‍jatipi vipaj‍jatipi|

‘‘ahañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ vamanaṃ daisaissāmi, yaṃ vamanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ vamanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamañ‍ca taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ vamanaṃ, yaṃ vamanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ vamanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti…pai॰… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā  sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti?

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi vantā hauti; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa vantā haunti; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau vantau hauti…pai॰… sammāvācassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā vantā hauti… sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau vantau hauti… sammāājīvassa bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau vantau hauti… sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau vantau hauti… sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati vantā hauti… sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi vantau hauti… sammāñāṇissa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ vantaṃ hauti  …pai॰…|

‘‘sammāvimuttissa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti vantā hauti; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa vantā haunti; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| idaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ vamanaṃ yaṃ vamanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva nau vipaj‍jati, yaṃ vamanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti…pai॰… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍cantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. niddhamanīyasuttaṃ

110. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, niddhamanīyā dhammā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi niddhantā hauti; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa niddhantā haunti; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau niddhantau hauti…pai॰… sammāvācassa bhikkhavai, micchāvācā niddhantā hauti… sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau niddhantau hauti… sammāājīvassa, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau niddhantau hauti… sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau niddhantau hauti… sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati niddhantā hauti… sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi niddhantau hauti… sammāñāṇissa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ niddhantaṃ hauti…|

‘‘sammāvimuttissa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti niddhantā hauti; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā  dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa niddhantā haunti; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa niddhamanīyā dhammā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. paṭhamaasaikhasuttaṃ

111. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘asaikhau  asaikhau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā bhantai, bhikkhu asaikhau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu asaikhāya sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsaṅkappaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāvācāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammākammantaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāvāyāmaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāsatiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsamādhinā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāvimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bhikkhu asaikhau hautī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. dutiyaasaikhasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, asaikhiyā dhammā| katamai dasa? asaikhā sammādiṭṭhi, asaikhau sammāsaṅkappau, asaikhā sammāvācā, asaikhau sammākammantau, asaikhau sammāājīvau, asaikhau sammāvāyāmau, asaikhā sammāsati, asaikhau sammāsamādhi, asaikhaṃ sammāñāṇaṃ, asaikhā sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa asaikhiyā dhammā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

samaṇasañ‍ñāvaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sañ‍ñā  baujjhaṅgā micchattaṃ, bījaṃ vij‍jāya nij‍jaraṃ|

dhauvanaṃ tikicchā vamanaṃ niddhamanaṃ dvai asaikhāti||


(12) 2. pac‍caurauhaṇivaggau

1. paṭhamaadhammasuttaṃ

113. a॰ ni॰ 10.171 ‘‘adhammau  ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau anatthau ca; dhammau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā anatthañ‍ca, dhammañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau ca anatthau ca? micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇaṃ, micchāvimutti – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhammau ca anatthau ca|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dhammau ca atthau ca? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇaṃ, sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammau ca atthau ca|

‘‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau anatthau ca; dhammau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā anatthañ‍ca, dhammañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaadhammasuttaṃ

114. ‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā  atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau, katamau ca dhammau, katamau ca anatthau, katamau ca atthau?

‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammādiṭṭhi dhammau; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāsaṅkappau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāsaṅkappau dhammau; yai ca micchāsaṅkappapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāsaṅkappapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāvācā, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvācā dhammau; yai ca micchāvācāpac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāvācāpac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchākammantau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammākammantau dhammau; yai ca micchākammantapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ  anatthau; sammākammantapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāājīvau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāājīvau dhammau; yai ca micchāājīvapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāājīvapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāvāyāmau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvāyāmau dhammau; yai ca micchāvāyāmapac‍cayā  anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāvāyāmapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāsati, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāsati dhammau; yai ca micchāsatipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā  akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāsatipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāsamādhi, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāsamādhi dhammau; yai ca micchāsamādhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāsamādhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāñāṇaṃ dhammau; yai ca micchāñāṇapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāñāṇapac‍cayā  ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāvimutti, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvimutti dhammau; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyaadhammasuttaṃ

115. ‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca  vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā  paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti?

atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma pucchaiyyāma (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.202 passitabbaṃ| yathā nau āyasmā ānandau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau ānanda, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti|

‘‘taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina  uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – adhammau ca…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbanti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti?

‘‘taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau āyasmā ānandau byākarissati tathā naṃ  dhāraissāmā’ti| vibhajatu āyasmā ānandau’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva mūlaṃ atik‍kamma khandhaṃ sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; aivaṃsampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ tumhai bhagavantaṃyaiva  upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ  paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘addhāvusau ānanda, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ mayaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma, yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā ānandau agaruṃ katvā’’ti|

‘‘tainahāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa  pac‍cassausuṃ| athāyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti|

katamau cāvusau, adhammau, katamau ca dhammau, katamau ca anatthau, katamau ca atthau?

‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, āvusau, adhammau; sammādiṭṭhi dhammau; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā  akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāsaṅkappau, āvusau, adhammau; sammāsaṅkappau dhammau… micchāvācā, āvusau, adhammau; sammāvācā dhammau  … micchākammantau, āvusau, adhammau; sammākammantau dhammau… micchāājīvau, āvusau, adhammau; sammāājīvau  dhammau… micchāvāyāmau, āvusau, adhammau; sammāvāyāmau dhammau… micchāsati, āvusau, adhammau; sammāsati dhammau… micchāsamādhi, āvusau, adhammau; sammāsamādhi dhammau… micchāñāṇaṃ, āvusau, adhammau; sammāñāṇaṃ dhammau…|

micchāvimutti, āvusau, adhammau; sammāvimutti dhammau; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘ayaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina  atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai, āvusau, bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākarauti byākaraiyya (syā॰) tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰… tathā paṭij‍jitabba’nti|

‘‘taisaṃ  nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina  atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰…  tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbanti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti?

‘‘taisaṃ  nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau āyasmā ānandau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’ti|

‘‘atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamimhā; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ apucchimhā| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā ānandaina imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau suvibhattau’’ti vibhattauti (?) aivamaiva hi añ‍ñaisu īdisasuttaisu dissati|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai! paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, ānandau| mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, ānandau| maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi caitaṃ aivamaivaṃ ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 1.205) byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ ānandaina byākataṃ| aisau caiva tassa aisau caivaitassa (ma॰ ni॰ 1.205) atthau aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ajitasuttaṃ

116. atha khau ajitau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ajitau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘amhākaṃ, bhau gautama, paṇḍitau nāma sabrahmacārī| taina pañ‍camattāni cittaṭṭhānasatāni cintitāni, yaihi añ‍ñatitthiyā upāraddhāva jānanti upāraddhā pajānanti (sī॰) upāraddhasmā’’ti upāraddhamhāti (sī॰ pī॰)|

atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dhāraitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitavatthūnī’’ti? ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā bhāsaiyya, bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau adhammikaina vādaina adhammikaṃ vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷaiti, taina ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañ‍jaiti| taina sā adhammikā parisā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā hauti – ‘paṇḍitau vata, bhau, paṇḍitau vata, bhau’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau adhammikaina vādaina dhammikaṃ vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷaiti, taina ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañ‍jaiti| taina sā adhammikā parisā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā hauti – ‘paṇḍitau vata, bhau, paṇḍitau vata, bhau’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau adhammikaina vādaina dhammikañ‍ca vādaṃ adhammikañ‍ca vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷaiti, taina ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañ‍jaiti| taina sā adhammikā parisā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā hauti – ‘paṇḍitau vata, bhau, paṇḍitau vata, bhau’ti|

‘‘adhammau  ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau, katamau ca dhammau, katamau ca anatthau, katamau ca atthau? micchādiṭṭhi, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammādiṭṭhi dhammau; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā  akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchāsaṅkappau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāsaṅkappau dhammau… micchāvācā, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvācā dhammau… micchākammantau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammākammantau dhammau… micchāājīvau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāājīvau dhammau … micchāvāyāmau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvāyāmau dhammau… micchāsati, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāsati dhammau… micchāsamādhi, bhikkhavai adhammau; sammāsamādhi dhammau… micchāñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāñāṇaṃ dhammau|

‘‘micchāvimutti, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammāvimutti dhammau; yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

117. a॰ ni॰ 10.169 atha khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, kiṃ pārimaṃ tīra’’nti? ‘‘micchādiṭṭhi khau, brāhmaṇa, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāsaṅkappau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāsaṅkappau pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāvācā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvācā pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchākammantau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammākammantau pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāājīvau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāājīvau pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāvāyāmau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvāyāmau pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāsati aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāsati pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāsamādhi aurimaṃ  tīraṃ, sammāsamādhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāñāṇaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāñāṇaṃ pārimaṃ tīraṃ; micchāvimutti aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvimutti pārimaṃ tīranti| idaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ  pārimaṃ tīranti|

‘‘appakā tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ  dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anaukamāgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ  sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānapaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau jutīmantau (sī॰), tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. aurimatīrasuttaṃ

118. ‘‘aurimañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tīraṃ daisaissāmi pārimañ‍ca tīraṃ| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, katamañ‍ca pārimaṃ tīraṃ? micchādiṭṭhi aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ…pai॰… micchāvimutti aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvimutti pārimaṃ tīraṃ| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīranti|

‘‘appakā tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai  ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anauka māgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānapaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau, tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamapac‍caurauhaṇīsuttaṃ

119. taina  khau pana samayaina jāṇussauṇi jānussauni (ka॰ sī॰), jānussauṇi (ka॰ sī॰), jāṇusauṇi (ka॰) brāhmaṇau tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātau sīsaṃnahātau (sī॰ pī॰), sīsanhātau (syā॰) navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthau al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ  ādāya bhagavatau avidūrai aikamantaṃ ṭhitau hauti|

addasā khau bhagavā jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātaṃ navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthaṃ al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ| disvāna jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātau navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthau al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya aikamantaṃ ṭhitau? kiṃ nvaj‍ja kiṃ nu aj‍ja (syā॰), kiṃ nu khau aj‍ja (pī॰), kiṃ nu khvaj‍ja (ka॰) brāhmaṇakulassā’’ti brāhmaṇa brahmakusalassāti (ka॰)? ‘‘pac‍caurauhaṇī, bhau gautama, aj‍ja brāhmaṇakulassā’’ti brahmakusalassāti (ka॰)|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātā navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthā al‍laina gaumayaina pathaviṃ aupuñ‍jitvā haritaihi kusaihi pattharitvā pavitthāraitvā (ka॰) antarā ca vailaṃ  antarā ca agyāgāraṃ saiyyaṃ kappainti| tai taṃ rattiṃ tikkhattuṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya pañ‍jalikā aggiṃ namassanti – ‘pac‍caurauhāma bhavantaṃ, pac‍caurauhāma bhavanta’nti| bahukaina ca sappitailanavanītaina aggiṃ santappainti| tassā ca rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina brāhmaṇai santappainti| aivaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti? sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘taina  hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘micchādiṭṭhiyā khau pāpakau vipākau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati; micchādiṭṭhiyā pac‍caurauhati|

… micchāsaṅkappassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāsaṅkappaṃ pajahati; micchāsaṅkappā pac‍caurauhati|

… micchāvācāya khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāvācaṃ pajahati; micchāvācāya pac‍caurauhati|

…micchākammantassa  khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchākammantaṃ pajahati; micchākammantā  pac‍caurauhati|

…micchāājīvassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāājīvaṃ pajahati; micchāājīvā pac‍caurauhati|

…micchāvāyāmassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāvāyāmaṃ pajahati; micchāvāyāmā pac‍caurauhati|

…micchāsatiyā khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāsatiṃ pajahati; micchāsatiyā pac‍caurauhati|

…micchāsamādhissa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāsamādhiṃ pajahati; micchāsamādhimhā pac‍caurauhati|

…micchāñāṇassa  khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāñāṇaṃ pajahati; micchāñāṇamhā pac‍caurauhati|

‘micchāvimuttiyā khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāvimuttiṃ pajahati; micchāvimuttiyā pac‍caurauhati| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti| imissā ca, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇiyā brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyapac‍caurauhaṇīsuttaṃ

120. ‘‘ariyaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, pac‍caurauhaṇiṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha… katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ariyā pac‍caurauhaṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti  paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘micchādiṭṭhiyā khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati; micchādiṭṭhiyā pac‍caurauhati| micchāsaṅkappassa khau pāpakau vipākau… micchāvācāya khau… micchākammantassa khau… micchāājīvassa khau… micchāvāyāmassa khau… micchāsatiyā khau… micchāsamādhissa khau… micchāñāṇassa khau… micchāvimuttiyā khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchāvimuttiṃ pajahati; micchāvimuttiyā pac‍caurauhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyā pac‍caurauhaṇī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pubbaṅgamasuttaṃ

121. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi| sammādiṭṭhikassa, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau pahauti, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahauti, sammākammantau  pahauti, sammākammantassa sammāājīvau pahauti, sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmau pahauti, sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahauti, sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahauti, sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahauti, sammāñāṇissa sammāvimutti pahautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

122. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattanti| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇaṃ, sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

pac‍caurauhaṇivaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

tayau adhammā ajitau, saṅgāravau ca aurimaṃ|

dvai caiva pac‍caurauhaṇī, pubbaṅgamaṃ āsavakkhayauti||


(13) 3. parisuddhavaggau

1. paṭhamasuttaṃ

123. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇaṃ, sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasuttaṃ

124. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi …pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyasuttaṃ

125. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā mahapphalā mahānisaṃsā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā mahapphalā mahānisaṃsā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. catutthasuttaṃ

126. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā rāgavinayapariyausānā haunti dausavinayapariyausānā haunti mauhavinayapariyausānā haunti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā rāgavinayapariyausānā  haunti dausavinayapariyausānā haunti mauhavinayapariyausānā haunti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasuttaṃ

127. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasuttaṃ

128. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi …pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sattamasuttaṃ

129. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasuttaṃ

130. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā rāgavinayapariyausānā haunti dausavinayapariyausānā haunti mauhavinayapariyausānā haunti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā  rāgavinayapariyausānā haunti dausavinayapariyausānā haunti mauhavinayapariyausānā haunti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. navamasuttaṃ

131. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dasamasuttaṃ

132. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, micchattā| katamai dasa? micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇaṃ, micchāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa micchattā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. aikādasamasuttaṃ

133. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, sammattā| katamai dasa? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇaṃ, sammāvimutti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa sammattā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

parisuddhavaggau tatiyau|



(14) 4. sādhuvaggau

1. sādhusuttaṃ

134. a॰ ni॰ 10.178 ‘‘sādhuñ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asādhuñ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asādhu? micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇaṃ, micchāvimutti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asādhu| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sādhu? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇaṃ, sammāvimutti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sādhū’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ariyadhammasuttaṃ

135. ‘‘ariyadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anariyadhammañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anariyau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anariyau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyau dhammau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. akusalasuttaṃ

136. ‘‘akusalañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi kusalañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha  …pai॰… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, akusalaṃ? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, akusalaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kusalaṃ? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kusala’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. atthasuttaṃ

137. ‘‘atthañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anatthañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anatthau? micchādiṭṭhi …pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anatthau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, atthau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atthau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dhammasuttaṃ

138. ‘‘dhammañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi adhammañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sāsavasuttaṃ

139. ‘‘sāsavañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi anāsavañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sāsavau  dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāsavau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anāsavau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anāsavau dhammau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sāvaj‍jasuttaṃ

140. ‘‘sāvaj‍jañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi anavaj‍jañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sāvaj‍jau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāvaj‍jau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anavaj‍jau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anavaj‍jau dhammau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. tapanīyasuttaṃ

141. ‘‘tapanīyañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi atapanīyañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, tapanīyau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, tapanīyau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, atapanīyau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atapanīyau dhammau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ācayagāmisuttaṃ

142. ‘‘ācayagāmiñ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi apacayagāmiñ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ācayagāmī dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ācayagāmī dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, apacayagāmī dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, apacayagāmī dhammau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dukkhudrayasuttaṃ

143. ‘‘dukkhudrayañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi sukhudrayañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhudrayau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhudrayau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sukhudrayau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhudrayau dhammau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. dukkhavipākasuttaṃ

144. ‘‘dukkhavipākañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi sukhavipākañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhavipākau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhavipākau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sukhavipākau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhavipākau dhammau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

sādhuvaggau catutthau|



(15) 5. ariyavaggau

1. ariyamaggasuttaṃ

145. ‘‘ariyamaggañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi anariyamaggañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anariyau maggau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anariyau maggau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau maggau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyau maggau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaṇhamaggasuttaṃ

146. ‘‘kaṇhamaggañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi suk‍kamaggañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, kaṇhamaggau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kaṇhamaggau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, suk‍kamaggau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, suk‍kamaggau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saddhammasuttaṃ

147. ‘‘saddhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi asaddhammañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaddhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaddhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, saddhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhammau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sappurisadhammasuttaṃ

148. ‘‘sappurisadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asappurisadhammañ‍ca| taṃ  suṇātha  …pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. uppādaitabbasuttaṃ

149. ‘‘uppādaitabbañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na uppādaitabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na uppādaitabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na uppādaitabbau dhammau| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, uppādaitabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, uppādaitabbau dhammau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. āsaivitabbasuttaṃ

150. ‘‘āsaivitabbañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na āsaivitabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na āsaivitabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na āsaivitabbau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, āsaivitabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āsaivitabbau dhammau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bhāvaitabbasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘bhāvaitabbañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na bhāvaitabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na bhāvaitabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na bhāvaitabbau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitabbau dhammau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. bahulīkātabbasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘bahulīkātabbañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na bahulīkātabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na bahulīkātabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na bahulīkātabbau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bahulīkātabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bahulīkātabbau dhammau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. anussaritabbasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘anussaritabbañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na anussaritabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na anussaritabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na anussaritabbau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anussaritabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anussaritabbau dhammau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sacchikātabbasuttaṃ

154. ‘‘sacchikātabbañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi na sacchikātabbañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, na sacchikātabbau dhammau? micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, na sacchikātabbau dhammau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbau dhammau? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbau dhammau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ariyavaggau pañ‍camau|


tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



4. catutthapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(16) 1. puggalavaggau

1. saivitabbasuttaṃ

155. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau na saivitabbau| katamaihi dasahi? micchādiṭṭhikau  hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvācau hauti, micchākammantau hauti, micchāājīvau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsati hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti, micchāñāṇī hauti, micchāvimutti hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau na saivitabbau|

‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau saivitabbau| katamaihi dasahi? sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti, sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau saivitabbau’’ti|

2-12. bhajitabbādisuttāni

156-166. ‘‘dasahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau na bhajitabbau…pai॰… bhajitabbau…pai॰… na payirupāsitabbau… payirupāsitabbau…pai॰… na puj‍jau hauti… puj‍jau hauti…pai॰… na pāsaṃsau hauti… pāsaṃsau hauti…pai॰… agāravau hauti… sagāravau hauti…pai॰… appatissau hauti… sappatissau hauti…pai॰… na ārādhakau hauti  … ārādhakau hauti…pai॰… na visujjhati… visujjhati…pai॰… mānaṃ nādhibhauti… mānaṃ adhibhauti…pai॰ … pañ‍ñāya na vaḍḍhati… pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati…pai॰…|

‘‘bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ  pasavati… bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi dasahi? sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti, sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti|

puggalavaggau paṭhamau|



(17) 2. jāṇussauṇivaggau

1. brāhmaṇapac‍caurauhaṇīsuttaṃ

167. taina  khau pana samayaina jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātau navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthau al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya bhagavatau avidūrai aikamantaṃ ṭhitau hauti|

addasā khau bhagavā jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātaṃ navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthaṃ al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ| disvāna jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātau navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthau al‍lakusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya aikamantaṃ ṭhitau? kiṃ nvaj‍ja brāhmaṇakulassā’’ti? ‘‘pac‍caurauhaṇī, bhau gautama, aj‍ja brāhmaṇakulassā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā tadahupausathai sīsaṃnhātā navaṃ khaumayugaṃ nivatthā al‍laina gaumayaina pathaviṃ aupuñ‍jitvā haritaihi kusaihi pattharitvā antarā ca vailaṃ antarā ca agyāgāraṃ saiyyaṃ kappainti| tai taṃ rattiṃ tikkhattuṃ  pac‍cuṭṭhāya pañ‍jalikā aggiṃ namassanti – ‘pac‍caurauhāma bhavantaṃ, pac‍caurauhāma bhavanta’nti| bahukaina ca sappitailanavanītaina aggiṃ santappainti| tassā ca rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina brāhmaṇai santappainti| aivaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ  pana, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti? sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘taina  hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘pāṇātipātassa khau pāpakau vipākau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya pāṇātipātaṃ pajahati; pāṇātipātā pac‍caurauhati|

…adin‍nādānassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya adin‍nādānaṃ pajahati; adin‍nādānā pac‍caurauhati|

…kāmaisumicchācārassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pajahati; kāmaisumicchācārā pac‍caurauhati|

…musāvādassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya  musāvādaṃ pajahati; musāvādā pac‍caurauhati|

…pisuṇāya vācāya khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai  abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pajahati; pisuṇāya vācāya pac‍caurauhati|

…pharusāya vācāya khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya pharusaṃ vācaṃ pajahati; pharusāya vācāya pac‍caurauhati|

…samphappalāpassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya samphappalāpaṃ pajahati; samphappalāpā pac‍caurauhati|

…abhijjhāya  khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya abhijjhaṃ pajahati; abhijjhāya pac‍caurauhati|

…byāpādassa  khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cāti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya byāpādaṃ pajahati; byāpādā pac‍caurauhati|

…micchādiṭṭhiyā khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati; micchādiṭṭhiyā pac‍caurauhati| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hautī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇī hauti| imissā, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai pac‍caurauhaṇiyā brāhmaṇānaṃ pac‍caurauhaṇī kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ariyapac‍caurauhaṇīsuttaṃ

168. ‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, pac‍caurauhaṇiṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ariyā pac‍caurauhaṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘pāṇātipātassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya pāṇātipātaṃ pajahati; pāṇātipātā pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘adin‍nādānassa khau pāpakau vipākau – diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya adin‍nādānaṃ pajahati; adin‍nādānā pac‍caurauhati|

…  ‘kāmaisumicchācārassa khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārā pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘musāvādassa  khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… musāvādā pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘pisuṇāya vācāya khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… pisuṇāya vācāya pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘pharusāya vācāya khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… pharusāya vācāya pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘samphappalāpassa khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… samphappalāpā pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘abhijjhāya khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰… abhijjhāya pac‍caurauhati|

… ‘byāpādassa khau pāpakau vipākau…pai॰…  byāpādā pac‍caurauhati|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ariyā pac‍caurauhaṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘micchādiṭṭhiyā khau pāpakau vipākau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍cā’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati; micchādiṭṭhiyā pac‍caurauhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyā pac‍caurauhaṇī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

169. a॰ ni॰ 10.117 atha  khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, kiṃ pārimaṃ tīra’’nti? ‘‘pāṇātipātau khau, brāhmaṇa, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| adin‍nādānaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| kāmaisumicchācārau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| musāvādau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| pisuṇā vācā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| pharusā vācā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| samphappalāpau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| abhijjhā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, anabhijjhā pārimaṃ tīraṃ| byāpādau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, abyāpādau pārimaṃ tīraṃ| micchādiṭṭhi aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ| idaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīranti|

‘‘appakā  tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ  itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anaukamāgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ  sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānapaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau, tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. aurimasuttaṃ

170. ‘‘aurimañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tīraṃ daisaissāmi pārimañ‍ca tīraṃ| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, katamañ‍ca pārimaṃ tīraṃ? pāṇātipātau, bhikkhavai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| adin‍nādānaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| kāmaisumicchācārau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| musāvādau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| pisuṇā vācā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| pharusā vācā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| samphappalāpau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ| abhijjhā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, anabhijjhā pārimaṃ tīraṃ| byāpādau aurimaṃ tīraṃ, abyāpādau pārimaṃ  tīraṃ| micchādiṭṭhi aurimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīranti|

‘‘appakā  tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ  dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anaukamāgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ  sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānapaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau, tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaadhammasuttaṃ

171. a॰ ni॰ 10.113 ‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau anatthau ca; dhammau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā anatthañ‍ca, dhammañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau ca anatthau ca? pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau, musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau, abhijjhā, byāpādau, micchādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhammau  ca anatthau ca|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dhammau ca atthau ca? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī, anabhijjhā, abyāpādau, sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammau ca atthau ca|

‘‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau anatthau ca; dhammau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā anatthañ‍ca, dhammañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaadhammasuttaṃ

172. ‘‘adhammau  ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau  yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha  khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti?

atha  khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyāma| yathā nau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca  viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti|

‘‘taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – adhammau ca, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tathā  paṭipaj‍jitabbanti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina  uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti?

‘‘taisaṃ  nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’ti| vibhajatu āyasmā mahākac‍cānau’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāraṃ gavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva mūlaṃ atik‍kamma khandhaṃ sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya| aivaṃsampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha mañ‍ñaitha (sī॰), mañ‍ñaiyyātha (ka॰)| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ tumhai bhagavantaṃyaiva  upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘addhā, āvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa  dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ mayaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau agaruṃ karitvā’’ti|

‘‘taina  hāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| athāyasmā mahākac‍cānau aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti|

‘‘katamau, cāvusau, adhammau; katamau ca dhammau? katamau ca anatthau, katamau ca atthau? ‘‘pāṇātipātau, āvusau, adhammau; pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca pāṇātipātapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; pāṇātipātā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ, āvusau, adhammau; adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca adin‍nādānapac‍cayā anaikai  pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; adin‍nādānā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘kāmaisumicchācārau, āvusau, adhammau; kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca kāmaisumicchācārapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘musāvādau, āvusau, adhammau; musāvādā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca musāvādapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; musāvādā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘pisuṇā vācā, āvusau, adhammau; pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca pisuṇāvācāpac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘pharusā  vācā, āvusau, adhammau; pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca pharusāvācāpac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘samphappalāpau, āvusau, adhammau; samphappalāpā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca samphappalāpapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; samphappalāpā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ  atthau|

‘‘abhijjhā, āvusau, adhammau; anabhijjhā dhammau; yai ca abhijjhāpac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; anabhijjhāpac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘byāpādau, āvusau, adhammau; abyāpādau dhammau; yai ca byāpādapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; abyāpādapac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, āvusau, adhammau; sammādiṭṭhi dhammau; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ  avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti| imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai, āvusau, bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā nau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti|

‘‘taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau…pai॰… tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti?

‘‘taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa  vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’ti|

‘‘atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau  tainupasaṅkamimhā; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ apucchimhā| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina imaihi akkharaihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau suvibhattau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai! paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau| mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau| maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi caitaṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ mahākac‍cānaina byākataṃ| aisau caiva tassa atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyaadhammasuttaṃ

173. ‘‘adhammau  ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, adhammau, katamau ca dhammau; katamau ca anatthau, katamau ca atthau? pāṇātipātau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī dhammau; yai ca pāṇātipātapac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; pāṇātipātā vairamaṇipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhammau; adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī dhammau… kāmaisumicchācārau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī dhammau… musāvādau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; musāvādā vairamaṇī dhammau… pisuṇā vācā, bhikkhavai, adhammau; pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī dhammau… pharusā vācā, bhikkhavai, adhammau; pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī dhammau… samphappalāpau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; samphappalāpā vairamaṇī dhammau… abhijjhā, bhikkhavai, adhammau; anabhijjhā dhammau… byāpādau, bhikkhavai, adhammau; abyāpādau dhammau…|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, bhikkhavai, adhammau; sammādiṭṭhi dhammau; yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anatthau; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ atthau|

‘‘‘adhammau ca, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbau dhammau ca; anatthau ca vaiditabbau atthau ca| adhammañ‍ca viditvā dhammañ‍ca, anatthañ‍ca viditvā atthañ‍ca yathā dhammau yathā atthau tathā paṭipaj‍jitabba’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. kammanidānasuttaṃ

174. ‘‘pāṇātipātampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘adin‍nādānampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘kāmaisumicchācārampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘musāvādampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘pisuṇavācampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘pharusavācampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘samphappalāpampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘abhijjhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘byāpādampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tividhaṃ vadāmi – laubhahaitukampi, dausahaitukampi, mauhahaitukampi| iti khau, bhikkhavai, laubhau kammanidānasambhavau, dausau kammanidānasambhavau, mauhau kammanidānasambhavau| laubhakkhayā kammanidānasaṅkhayau, dausakkhayā kammanidānasaṅkhayau, mauhakkhayā kammanidānasaṅkhayau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. parik‍kamanasuttaṃ
